# Description rewrites: review

Each product: the rewrite, the original description as it was on the page, and every fact in the rewrite with where it came from. Sources: Description (the main copy), Description footnote (the * notes), Specifications / Extra Items / Pictures tabs, Options and Versions in the buy box, and the product title. FLAG marks anything to double-check.

## RTX-69C-CAM-X: 1969 Camaro RTX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1969-camaro-rtx-instruments-p1155.html` (product 1155)

### Rewrite

With the key off, it looks like the factory '69 cluster: big speedometer, big tach, stock layout and face styling. Turn the key and LED bar gauges light up for oil pressure, coolant temp, voltage and fuel. Two color message centers show what you choose, from trip meters and miles to empty to 0-60 times and quarter-mile trap speed.

It drops into your stock or reproduction plastic bezel (sold separately) and uses an OEM or reproduction instrument carrier. The stock lenses and indicator rings are replaced by matte black aluminum trim rings, included in the box. A metric version in km/h and Celsius is a special order.

The housings are CNC machined and the electronics are designed and built in-house in Sioux Falls. Everything wires to one control box, so install and setup stay simple.

### Original

> A drop-in upgrade for your stock or reproduction plastic bezel (available separately), this package will have even the most diehard Camaro fanatic fooled. With the key off, the look is all but OEM with a large speedometer and tach, but adding power changes everything. Segmented LED bars come alive to display oil pressure, coolant temperature, voltage and fuel level and the twin TFT message centers display a full array of information. Stock lenses and indicator rings are replaced with matte black aluminum trim rings (supplied).
>
> RTX keeps the stock look and adds the late-model features you want. The layout, faces and indicators follow the original design so closely that most people will take it for factory, right up until you turn the key and the LED backlighting comes on in your colors.
>
> Every RTX housing is CNC machined, and the electronics are designed and built in-house. The high-definition TFT message centers show just about any reading you want, and one central control box keeps installation and setup simple. Full details are in the sections below.
>
> *Gauge trim rings included. System uses OEM or reproduction style Instrument Carrier Assembly.

## RTX-55C-X: 1955-56 Chevy Car RTX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1955-56-chevy-car-rtx-instruments-p1134.html` (product 1134)

### Rewrite

Even the mesh pattern behind the speedometer needle matches the stock 1955-56 Chevy cluster. Most people will take it for factory until you turn the key. Then the LED backlighting comes on in the colors you pick, and a full-color TFT message center shows trip meters, miles to empty and 0-60 times.

You'll use your stock or reproduction bezel, which isn't included. The system takes the place of the stock automatic gear indicator above the steering column, so add the GSS-3000 sensor (sold separately) if you want gear position on the screen. The matching RLC-55C clock finishes off the passenger side. A metric version is a special order.

Every RTX housing is CNC machined, and the electronics are designed and built in-house. One central control box keeps installation and setup simple.

### Original

> This 21st century wonder has the look of a stock 1955-56 Chevy cluster but offers a full-color TFT message center and LED backlighting (user-adjustable colors). The casual observer will think this is vintage stuff, right down to the mesh pattern behind the speedometer needle – until you hit the key! Use a stock or reproduction bezel; available separately. Add RLC-55C clock to finish off the passenger side.
>
> RTX keeps the stock look and adds the late-model features you want. The layout, faces and indicators follow the original design so closely that most people will take it for factory, right up until you turn the key and the LED backlighting comes on in your colors.
>
> Every RTX housing is CNC machined, and the electronics are designed and built in-house. The high-definition TFT message centers show just about any reading you want, and one central control box keeps installation and setup simple. Full details are in the sections below.
>
> *Bezel not included, shown for visual/presentation purposes. System uses OEM or reproduction style bezel. * Instrument system eliminates the stock automatic gear shift indicator immediately above steering column. To display gear position, add GSS-3000 Gear Shift Sensor (sold separately)

## HDX-55C: 1955-56 Chevy Car HDX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1955-56-chevy-car-hdx-instruments-p1033.html` (product 1033)

### Rewrite

Six analog gauges fill the stock '55-56 Chevy dash, and a large TFT display adds more readings on top of them. Pick black or silver gauge faces. Then set your own lighting colors for the readings, the needles and the message center.

It installs in your stock or reproduction bezel. The bezel isn't included, and reproduction chrome pieces are sold separately. So is the HLC clock, which is made to go with this system and isn't meant to be used alone. The system replaces the stock automatic gear indicator above the steering column, so add a gear shift sensor (sold separately) to show gear position. A metric version is a special order.

Setup uses touch buttons in the face or the Bluetooth app for Apple and Android. A central control box takes signals from the car's harness and connects the included solid-state senders, which makes the install much simpler.

### Original

> The star of our favorite movies, the '55 and '56 Chevy cars are among the most customized in history. As such, this was one of the first direct-fit applications offered by Dakota Digital, so it was only natural to create an HDX system for the icon. A large TFT display lets you expand upon the six analog gauge readings, all installing in to your stock or reproduction bezel; repop chrome pieces are available separately, as is the HLC clock. Not to be used alone, the HLC is the perfect complement to the bolt-in HDX system. Available with your choice of black or silver gauge-face styling, each HDX system allows the user to select independent illumination colors for the gauge readings and needles, as well as the message centers. Calibration and personal settings are manipulated with the built-in, capacitive-touch buttons, or the available Bluetooth app for Apple and Android devices. Each needle hub is black nickel plated spun-aluminum to match the buttons. All HDX instrument systems feature dead-faced indicators (turn signals, high beam, check engine, cruise control, parking brake, plus two user-definable), and a red, settable warning light for each gauge. With our central control box, installation is greatly simplified with vehicle-harness sourced signals and supplied, solid state sending units connecting cleanly to their respective terminal.
>
> *Bezel not included, shown for visual/presentation purposes. System uses OEM or reproduction style bezel. * Instrument system eliminates the stock automatic gear shift indicator immediately above steering column. To display gear position, add GSS-2000 Gear Shift Sensor (sold separately)

## HDX-67C-PU: 1967-72 Chevy/ GMC Pickup HDX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1967-72-chevy-gmc-pickup-hdx-instruments-p1039.html` (product 1039)

### Rewrite

Every opening in the original seven-hole '67-72 GM pickup cluster gets put to work. Six analog gauges and a clock fill the holes, and twin TFT message centers give you trip meters, miles to empty and 0-60 times. You pick black or silver alloy faces and your own lighting colors.

It fits your stock or reproduction dash bezel, which is sold separately. A replacement bezel for these trucks is listed with this kit. A metric version in km/h and Celsius is a special order.

Touch buttons on the face, or the Bluetooth app for iPhone and Android, handle setup. The needle hubs are black nickel-plated spun aluminum to match the buttons. Each gauge has its own red warning light you can set. A central control box takes signals from the truck's harness and connects the included solid-state senders, so the install stays simple.

### Original

> The high-water mark of classic trucks, 1967-72 GM pickups have been, and always will be, the favorite. The original seven-hole cluster offers plenty of room, so why not fill it with the best? Six analog gauges plus a clock and twin TFT message centers make use of the space, ensuring the driver is in the know. System fits stock or reproduction dash bezel, available separately.
>
> HDX comes with black or silver alloy faces, and you choose separate lighting colors for the readings, needles and message centers. Set it up with the built-in touch buttons or the Bluetooth app for iPhone and Android. Needle hubs are black nickel-plated spun aluminum to match the buttons. Indicators stay hidden until they light up (turn signals, high beam, check engine, cruise control, parking brake and two you define), and every gauge has its own settable red warning light. A central control box takes signals from the vehicle harness and connects the included solid-state senders, which keeps installation simple. Full details are in the sections below.
>
> *Bezel not included; it's shown for presentation. The system uses your OEM or reproduction bezel.

## VHX-55C: 1955-56 Chevy Car VHX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1955-56-chevy-car-vhx-instruments-p719.html` (product 719)

### Rewrite

The '55-56 Chevy dash doesn't tell you much from the factory. This VHX system keeps that iconic cluster and fills it with six analog needles that light up, plus two LCD message centers. The faces are backlit, and the LCDs adjust their contrast so you can read them in bright sun.

Use your factory or reproduction bezel. It's sold separately. The factory gear indicator goes away, so add the GSS-3000 if you want the gear shown in words in the message centers. Faces come in black alloy, silver alloy or a carbon fiber look, and you can order blue or red. A matching clock, analog or digital, is made for VHX gauges. A metric version is a special order.

Precision stepper motors drive the needles, and the sensors are solid state. It's engineered and built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, and carries a limited lifetime warranty.

### Original

> The 1955-56 Chevy car dash is lacking in information; VHX-55C makes use of the iconic factory gauge cluster, filling it out with six analog readouts on top of dual LCD message centers. Able to be used with factory or reproduction bezels (sold separately). Factory gear-indicator is eliminated; use GSS-3000 for full gear-word readouts in the message centers.
>
> VHX gives you real needles that light up, backlit faces and easy-to-read LCD message centers. Underneath are micro-controlled precision stepper motors, solid-state sensors and displays you can customize. Like every Dakota Digital system, VHX is engineered and built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, and carries a limited lifetime warranty.
>
> *Bezel not included; it's shown for presentation. The system uses your OEM or reproduction bezel.
>
> Instrument system eliminates the stock automatic gear shift indicator immediately above steering column. To display gear position, add GSS-3000 Gear Shift Sensor (sold separately)

## VHX-69C-CAM: 1969 Camaro VHX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1969-camaro-vhx-instruments-p722.html` (product 722)

### Rewrite

Lit needles and backlit faces bring a 1969 Camaro dash up to today's standards. The LCD message centers show trip meters, 0-60 times and quarter-mile times, and you can customize what they display.

The system fits right into the stock Camaro gauge carrier and keeps your factory bezel. New clear lenses come in the kit, so the stock lenses aren't used. If you need a carrier, a reproduction one is sold separately. Faces come in black alloy, silver alloy or a carbon fiber look, in blue or red. A metric version is a special order.

Micro-controlled stepper motors move the needles, and the sensors are solid state. The included pushbuttons calibrate the speedometer and fuel gauge. VHX is engineered and built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, and carries a limited lifetime warranty.

### Original

> Arguably the pinnacle of GM Muscle Cars, the 1969 Camaro is the perfect vehicle for modification and bringing its features to today’s standards. These VHX Systems fit directly into the stock Camaro gauge carrier and include new clear lenses, the stock instrument lenses will no longer be used.
>
> VHX gives you real needles that light up, backlit faces and easy-to-read LCD message centers. Underneath are micro-controlled precision stepper motors, solid-state sensors and displays you can customize. Like every Dakota Digital system, VHX is engineered and built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, and carries a limited lifetime warranty.
>
> *Uses factory bezel. Reproduction Instrument Carrier Assembly sold separately.

## GRFX-73C-PU: GRFX-73C-PU: 1973-87 Chevy Pickup, 1973-91 Chevy Blazer, GMC Jimmy and Suburbans GRFX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/grfx-73c-pu-1973-87-chevy-pickup-1973-91-chevy-blazer-gmc-ji-p1357.html` (product 1357)

### Rewrite

Each gauge spot in this cluster can show an analog, digital or bar-graph readout, and you decide which. Start from a graphic theme, then set colors and warning modes for each piece. The largest screen can also show a backup camera, and a built-in G-force meter shows what's happening when you corner, launch and brake.

It bolts directly into 1973-87 Chevy pickups and 1973-91 Chevy Blazer, GMC Jimmy and Suburban dashes, and it works with any drivetrain. Backup cameras are sold separately and come with a full-length harness and a universal mount. The sensors are in the box, each with a full-length harness.

Built-in GPS calibrates the speedometer for your tire size and gearing, so there's nothing to set by hand. Bluetooth links the Dakota Digital app for setup and troubleshooting, and a USB port takes updates. A separate control module keeps wiring at the cluster tidy.

### Original

> A direct bolt-in to the '73-87 Chevy Truck, the GRFX-73C-PU offers the boldest interior upgrade that can be made. Bringing integration ability with any drivetrain, modern features and technology, along with the ability to customize to your heart's content, there is no comparison!
>
> With GRFX you can change nearly everything: pick a graphic theme, then set colors and warning modes for each element. Each gauge position can show an analog, digital or bar-graph readout, one reading at a time, two at once, or a different reading entirely.
>
> GRFX can also show a backup camera on its largest screen, right in the cluster. Dakota Digital backup cameras are sold separately and come with a full-length harness and a universal mount.
>
> Built-in GPS calibrates the speedometer automatically, whatever your tire size or gearing. A wired speed signal keeps the reading live while GPS serves as the reference, so there's nothing to calibrate by hand. Bluetooth connects the Dakota Digital app on your phone or tablet for setup, calibration, customization and troubleshooting with live gauge and sensor data. A USB port takes updates, including new graphic themes and future features.
>
> A built-in G-force meter shows the forces at work as you corner, launch and brake.
>
> Like every Dakota Digital system, it uses a separate control module, so wiring stays tidy and less of it runs to the cluster. The sensors are included, each with a full-length harness: a water temperature probe with adapter bushings, a solid-state oil pressure sender and a speedometer pulse generator.
>
> Every Dakota Digital instrument system is engineered and built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, and carries a limited lifetime warranty.

## GRFX-67C-PU: GRFX-67C-PU: 1967-72 Chevy Pickup GRFX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/grfx-67c-pu-1967-72-chevy-pickup-grfx-instruments-p1387.html` (product 1387)

### Rewrite

The GRFX cluster fills your 1967-72 C10 bezel with graphic gauges you can set up your way. Every gauge position can show one reading, two at once, or a different reading entirely, as a needle, a number or a bar graph. Choose a theme, then pick the colors and warning modes yourself.

It works with the original engine or the latest fuel injection you put under the hood. A replacement bezel for these trucks is listed with this kit. The largest screen can show a backup camera, sold separately. The included sensors each come with a full-length harness.

GPS inside the cluster calibrates the speedometer for your tires and gears on its own. Use the Dakota Digital app over Bluetooth for setup and live sensor data, and load updates through the USB port. It's engineered and built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, and carries a limited lifetime warranty.

### Original

> Fill up that 1967-72 C10 bezel up with the all new GRFX series from Dakota Digital. Compatible with any original engine or the latest fuel injection you want to put under the hood, this kit will provide detailed monitoring of your investment, while providing your eyes with the latest in technology and customization ability.
>
> With GRFX you can change nearly everything: pick a graphic theme, then set colors and warning modes for each element. Each gauge position can show an analog, digital or bar-graph readout, one reading at a time, two at once, or a different reading entirely.
>
> GRFX can also show a backup camera on its largest screen, right in the cluster. Dakota Digital backup cameras are sold separately and come with a full-length harness and a universal mount.
>
> Built-in GPS calibrates the speedometer automatically, whatever your tire size or gearing. A wired speed signal keeps the reading live while GPS serves as the reference, so there's nothing to calibrate by hand. Bluetooth connects the Dakota Digital app on your phone or tablet for setup, calibration, customization and troubleshooting with live gauge and sensor data. A USB port takes updates, including new graphic themes and future features.
>
> A built-in G-force meter shows the forces at work as you corner, launch and brake.
>
> Like every Dakota Digital system, it uses a separate control module, so wiring stays tidy and less of it runs to the cluster. The sensors are included, each with a full-length harness: a water temperature probe with adapter bushings, a solid-state oil pressure sender and a speedometer pulse generator.
>
> Every Dakota Digital instrument system is engineered and built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, and carries a limited lifetime warranty.

## VFD3-36C: 1935 Chevy Master/1936 Chevy Digital Instrument System

Page: `redesign/shop/1935-chevy-master-1936-chevy-digital-instrument-system-p97.html` (product 97)

### Rewrite

Bright digital readouts replace the gauges in your 1935 Chevy Master or 1936 Chevy. This Series III system uses vacuum fluorescent displays that are easy to read, in blue or teal. You get six gauges, and the message centers add an odometer, two trip meters, a 0-60 timer and quarter-mile time and trap speed.

The kit includes a billet aluminum dash housing made to fit the 1935 Master and 1936 Chevy. Water temperature, oil pressure and speed sensors are included, each with a 10 ft harness. A matching glove box door is sold separately, with or without a VFD clock. A metric version is a special order.

You set it up with pushbuttons you mount where you can reach them. They calibrate the speedometer and fuel gauge and set the cylinder count. Later, the same buttons scroll through the message centers.

### Original

> Series III systems use bright vacuum fluorescent displays: easy to read, accurate, and built for years of trouble-free driving.
>
> Full 6 Gauge Instrument System includes Billet Aluminum dash housing to fit 1935 Master and 1936 Chevy.

## VFD3-34F: 1933 -1934 Ford Digital Instrument System

### Rewrite

Your 1933-34 Ford gets bright vacuum fluorescent readouts in blue or teal for speed, rpm, oil pressure, water temp, fuel and volts. The message centers add an odometer, two trip meters, a 0-60 timer and quarter-mile time and trap speed.

A billet aluminum dash housing made for the '33-34 Ford comes with it. The glove box cover, center panel and glove box handle are sold separately, and the covers may need some modification. A complete kit option adds a VFD clock and the glove box cover with handle. A metric version is a special order.

Water temperature and oil pressure sensors are in the box, plus a speed sensor that replaces a Ford or GM style speedometer cable. Pushbuttons you mount within reach handle calibration and scroll the message centers.

## RTX-57C-X: 1957 Chevy Car RTX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1957-chevy-car-rtx-instruments-p1136.html` (product 1136)

### Rewrite

Only three gauges show on this stock-looking '57 Chevy cluster, but there are six readings inside. The tach, oil pressure and voltage are hidden LED bars that only appear when the key is on. A large TFT message center sits front and center, while the rest of the cluster looks straight out of 1957.

It fits into your original cast housing. The gauge pod and chrome inserts aren't included, so you'll use OEM or reproduction parts. Replacement chrome inserts and the matching RLC-57C clock are sold separately. A metric version is a special order.

Every RTX housing is CNC machined, and the electronics are designed and built in-house. One central control box keeps installation and setup simple, and the LED backlighting comes on in the colors you choose.

## HDX-57C: 1957 Chevy Car HDX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1957-chevy-car-hdx-instruments-p1035.html` (product 1035)

### Rewrite

The triple-round '57 Chevy cluster holds six analog gauge readings and a big TFT display in this HDX system. You can check nearly any drivetrain reading without giving up that famous dash. Choose black or silver faces, then set separate lighting colors for the readings, needles and message centers.

It installs in the cast-metal housing, which is required. The gauge pod and chrome inserts aren't included, so you'll use OEM or reproduction pieces. Reproduction chrome trim is sold separately. Add the matching HLC-57C clock for the passenger side. It only works with an HDX system. A metric version is a special order.

Touch buttons in the face, or the Bluetooth app for Apple and Android, handle calibration and settings. A central control box takes signals from the car's harness, so the install is much simpler.

### Original

> Transcending the hot rodding world, the ’57 Chevy car has become an American icon. Big tail fins, rockets on the hood and V8 power spells freedom on four wheels. For us, the triple-round gauge cluster is the perfect place to put six analog gauge readings, along with a big TFT display. You can view nearly any drivetrain parameter without sacrificing the world-class dashboard. Reproduction chrome trim is available separately; must use cast-metal housing for installation. Don’t forget to add the matching HLC clock for the passenger side. The HLC-57C clock cannot be used without an HDX system.
>
> Available with your choice of black or silver gauge-face styling, each HDX system allows the user to select independent illumination colors for the gauge readings and needles, as well as the message centers. Calibration and personal settings are manipulated with the built-in, capacitive-touch buttons, or the available Bluetooth app for Apple and Android devices. Each needle hub is black nickel plated spun-aluminum to match the buttons. All HDX instrument systems feature dead-faced indicators (turn signals, high beam, check engine, cruise control, parking brake, plus two user-definable), and a red, settable warning light for each gauge. With our central control box, installation is greatly simplified with vehicle-harness sourced signals and supplied, solid state sending units connecting cleanly to their respective terminal.
>
> *Gauge Pod and Chrome inserts not included, shown for visual/ presentation purposes. System use OEM or reproduction style bezel.

## HDX-47C-PU: 1947-53 Chevy/ GMC Pickup HDX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1947-53-chevy-gmc-pickup-hdx-instruments-p1036.html` (product 1036)

### Rewrite

No adapter rings or tiny round gauges here. Chrome-plated, machined aluminum housings replace the stock bezels and fit exactly into a stock 1947-53 Chevy or GMC pickup dash. Each gauge has a full-color TFT display and a pair of touch buttons, so you can set it up your way.

The machined aluminum and chrome bezel is included. The system needs a 5.2 inch opening and measures 5.95 inches overall, and the depth usually matches the stock cluster. Fitting it to a different vehicle? Make any changes to the dash, not the HDX system, to keep warranty coverage. Faces come in black or silver alloy. A metric version is a special order.

You can also set it up with the Bluetooth app for iPhone and Android. A central control box takes signals from the truck's harness and connects the included solid-state senders.

### Original

> Don’t settle for adapter rings and tiny round gauges crammed inside, go for a direct drop-in solution instead! Chrome plated, machined aluminum instrument housings replace the stock bezels for an exact fit into stock 1947-53 Chevy and GMC pickup dashboards. A pair of capacitive-touch buttons and a full-color TFT display in each gauge allows full personalization.
>
> HDX comes with black or silver alloy faces, and you choose separate lighting colors for the readings, needles and message centers. Set it up with the built-in touch buttons or the Bluetooth app for iPhone and Android. Needle hubs are black nickel-plated spun aluminum to match the buttons. Indicators stay hidden until they light up (turn signals, high beam, check engine, cruise control, parking brake and two you define), and every gauge has its own settable red warning light. A central control box takes signals from the vehicle harness and connects the included solid-state senders, which keeps installation simple. Full details are in the sections below.
>
> *Machined aluminum and chrome bezel included.

## GRFX-55C-PU: GRFX-55C-PU: 1955-59 Chevy Pickup, GRFX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/grfx-55c-pu-1955-59-chevy-pickup-grfx-instruments-p1359.html` (product 1359)

### Rewrite

A backlit billet aluminum knob in the face of this cluster runs the whole show. Turn it to change readouts and reset timers, or go deeper for calibration and display setup. Each gauge position can show an analog, digital or bar-graph readout, and you pick the graphic theme and colors.

It drops into the OEM front gauge bezel of a 1955-59 Chevy truck. The bezel isn't included, so use an OEM or reproduction one. It works with anything from the original engine to the latest LT power. The sensors come with full-length harnesses, and backup cameras are sold separately.

Built-in GPS calibrates the speedometer for your tires and gearing. Bluetooth connects the Dakota Digital app, and a USB port takes updates. It's engineered and built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, with a limited lifetime warranty.

### Original

> Dropping into the OEM front gauge bezel, the GRFX-55C-PU brings your 1955-59 Chevy Truck into the modern age. With a range of compatibility from the OEM engine to the latest LT power, this kit will provide detailed monitoring of your under-hood investment, while providing your eyes with the latest in technology and customization ability.
>
> With GRFX you can change nearly everything: pick a graphic theme, then set colors and warning modes for each element. Each gauge position can show an analog, digital or bar-graph readout, one reading at a time, two at once, or a different reading entirely.
>
> GRFX can also show a backup camera on its largest screen, right in the cluster. Dakota Digital backup cameras are sold separately and come with a full-length harness and a universal mount.
>
> Built-in GPS calibrates the speedometer automatically, whatever your tire size or gearing. A wired speed signal keeps the reading live while GPS serves as the reference, so there's nothing to calibrate by hand. Bluetooth connects the Dakota Digital app on your phone or tablet for setup, calibration, customization and troubleshooting with live gauge and sensor data. A USB port takes updates, including new graphic themes and future features.
>
> A built-in G-force meter shows the forces at work as you corner, launch and brake.
>
> The primary user-control is made with a fully-backlit rotary encoder. Conveniently located in the face of the instrument system, this billet-aluminum control knob provides access to readout changes, resetting of various timers or settings. Deeper yet, all calibration and display configuration can be done here as well, putting all system controls at the tip of your fingers!
>
> Like every Dakota Digital system, it uses a separate control module, so wiring stays tidy and less of it runs to the cluster. The sensors are included, each with a full-length harness: a water temperature probe with adapter bushings, a solid-state oil pressure sender and a speedometer pulse generator.
>
> Every Dakota Digital instrument system is engineered and built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, and carries a limited lifetime warranty.
>
> *Bezel not included; it's shown for presentation. The system uses your OEM or reproduction bezel.

## VFD3-33C-S: 1933-35 Chevy Standard Digital Instrument System

Page: `redesign/shop/1933-35-chevy-standard-digital-instrument-system-p39.html` (product 39)

### Rewrite

This 1933-35 Chevy Standard system shows six gauges on bright vacuum fluorescent displays in blue or teal. They're easy to read, and the message centers add an odometer, dual trip meters, a 0-60 timer and quarter-mile time and trap speed.

A billet aluminum dash housing made to fit the 1933-35 Chevy Standard comes in the kit. Water temperature, oil pressure and speed sensors are included, each with a 10 ft harness. The speed sensor needs about 5 inches of clearance behind the cable drive. A metric version is a special order.

Mount the included pushbuttons where you can reach them. They calibrate the speedometer and fuel gauge, set the cylinder count and then scroll through the message centers.

### Original

> Series III systems use bright vacuum fluorescent displays: easy to read, accurate, and built for years of trouble-free driving.
>
> Full 6 Gauge Instrument System includes Billet Aluminum dash housing to fit 1933- 35 Chevy Standard.

## VFD3-37C: 1937-38 Chevy Digital Instrument System

Page: `redesign/shop/1937-38-chevy-digital-instrument-system-p98.html` (product 98)

### Rewrite

Pick blue or teal for the vacuum fluorescent readouts in this 1937-38 Chevy system. Speed, rpm, oil pressure, water temp, fuel and volts all show in bright digits that are easy to see. The message centers add trip meters, a 0-60 timer and quarter-mile times.

A billet aluminum dash housing made for the 1937-38 Chevy is included. Matching aluminum dash panels and a glove box door are sold separately. The dash panel comes for a shaft or DIN radio, with or without A/C vents. The glove box door comes plain or with a digital clock. Its handle is separate, and the covers may need some modification. A metric version is a special order.

Water temperature, oil pressure and speed sensors come in the box with 10 ft harnesses. Pushbuttons you mount within reach handle calibration and the message centers.

## RTX-55C-PU-X: 1955-59 Chevy Pickup RTX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1955-59-chevy-pickup-rtx-instruments-p1135.html` (product 1135)

### Rewrite

It looks like a stock take-out cluster from a 1955-59 Chevy pickup, but it's all new and fully electronic. A tachometer now matches the small gauges up top, and the speedometer reads to 120 mph. A single TFT message center is set into a mesh pattern behind the speedometer needle, and the LED backlighting comes on in your colors with the key.

It fits your stock or reproduction chrome bezel, which is sold separately. It doesn't fit GMC trucks. A metric version is a special order.

Every RTX housing is CNC machined, and the electronics are designed and built in-house. Water temperature, oil pressure and speed sensors come in the box, and one central control box keeps setup simple.

### Original

> Make ‘em do a double take! What looks like a stock take-out gauge cluster from a 1955-59 Chevy pickup is actually an all-new, fully electronic technological wonder! We snuck in a tachometer to match the small gauges up top and bumped the speedo to 120mph and finished it off with a single TFT message center surrounded by a mesh pattern behind the speedometer needle. Fits stock or reproduction chrome bezel, available separately. Sorry, not for GMC.
>
> RTX keeps the stock look and adds the late-model features you want. The layout, faces and indicators follow the original design so closely that most people will take it for factory, right up until you turn the key and the LED backlighting comes on in your colors.
>
> Every RTX housing is CNC machined, and the electronics are designed and built in-house. The high-definition TFT message centers show just about any reading you want, and one central control box keeps installation and setup simple. Full details are in the sections below.
>
> *Bezel not included; it's shown for presentation. The system uses your OEM or reproduction bezel.

## RTX-73C-PU-X: 1973-87 Chevy Pickup, 1973-91 Chevy Blazer, GMC Jimmy and Suburbans RTX Instruments

### Rewrite

Pick the face that matches your square-body: 1973-75, 1976-78 or 1979-87 styling. All three keep the Chevy and GMC look, with late-model features underneath. Twin TFT message centers look like a vintage tumbler odometer but can add extra readings, and the LED backlighting comes on in your colors.

It bolts in with your stock or aftermarket front bezel, which isn't supplied. It also fits 1973-91 Chevy Blazer, GMC Jimmy and Suburban. Factory turn signal indicators switch to LED lighting. Keep the OEM gear indicator, or show gear position digitally by adding a GSS-3000. Metric versions are a special order.

Housings are CNC machined, and the electronics are designed and built in-house. One central control box keeps installation and setup simple.

### Original

> Paying tribute to all three gauge designs available over the years (1973-75, 1976-78, 1979-87 Pickups and '73-91 Blazer, Jimmy and Suburban), the RTX series maintains the beloved Chevy and GMC square-body look while bringing in late-model elements. A bolt-in affair with your stock or aftermarket front bezel (not supplied), the twin TFT message centers give the look of a vintage tumbler odometer, but can be expanded with auxiliary readings to keep you informed. Factory turn signal indicators will be converted to modern LED lighting, and the OEM gear-indicator can be retained, or can be shown digitally with the addition of a GSS-3000.
>

## HDX-55C-PU: 1955-59 Chevy Pickup HDX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1955-59-chevy-pickup-hdx-instruments-p1037.html` (product 1037)

### Rewrite

Builders have put the V-shaped 1955-59 Chevy pickup cluster in plenty of custom dashes, and it still looks right in the trucks it came in. This HDX system fills it with six analog gauges and a single TFT display in the center that shows exactly the information you want. You pick black or silver alloy faces and separate lighting colors for the readings, needles and message centers.

Mount it with a stock or reproduction bezel, sold separately. It doesn't fit GMC. A metric version is a special order.

Set it up with the built-in touch buttons or the Bluetooth app for iPhone and Android. Indicators stay hidden until they light, and each gauge has its own red warning light you can set. A central control box connects the included solid-state senders, which keeps the install simple.

### Original

> This V-shaped instrument cluster has found its way into more than a few custom dashboards, and still looks good in the 1955-59 Chevy pickup trucks it came in! Use a stock or reproduction bezel (available separately) to mount this all-inclusive package. A single TFT display in the center allows the user to see exactly the information they desire. Does not fit GMC.
>
> HDX comes with black or silver alloy faces, and you choose separate lighting colors for the readings, needles and message centers. Set it up with the built-in touch buttons or the Bluetooth app for iPhone and Android. Needle hubs are black nickel-plated spun aluminum to match the buttons. Indicators stay hidden until they light up (turn signals, high beam, check engine, cruise control, parking brake and two you define), and every gauge has its own settable red warning light. A central control box takes signals from the vehicle harness and connects the included solid-state senders, which keeps installation simple. Full details are in the sections below.
>
> *Bezel not included; it's shown for presentation. The system uses your OEM or reproduction bezel.

## VHX-47C-PU: 1947-53 Chevy/ GMC Pickup VHX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1947-53-chevy-gmc-pickup-vhx-instruments-p723.html` (product 723)

### Rewrite

The dual-round gauge layout in 1947-53 Chevy and GMC trucks has always made it hard to get more information. This VHX system puts six analog instruments and two LCD message centers in those two openings. The needles are fully lit, the faces are backlit, and the LCDs are easy to see.

Custom-machined chrome bezels are included and fit directly into the stock gauge openings of both Chevy and GMC trucks. The system needs a 5.2 inch opening and measures 5.95 inches overall. Faces come in black alloy, silver alloy or a carbon fiber look, in blue or red. A metric version is a special order.

Micro-controlled stepper motors move the needles, and the sensors are solid state. It's engineered and built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, and carries a limited lifetime warranty.

### Original

> These classic trucks strike a nerve with people everywhere, yet the simple dual-round gauge layout has long posed a challenge for rodders seeking more information. Dakota Digital offers six analog instruments and two LCD message centers in custom-machined and chromed bezels that fit directly into the stock gauge openings of both Chevy and GMC trucks.
>
> Fully lit needles, backlit faces, and highly visible LCD message centers are a few of the things that you can see which set the VHX Series apart from yesterday’s traditional approach to instrumentation. A few of the features which you don’t normally see are the micro-controlled precision stepper motors, solid state sensors for unparalleled accuracy, and user-customizable display feedback, all supported by an unrivaled limited lifetime warranty which has become the standard for Dakota Digital instrumentation systems . Completely engineered and manufactured at our facility in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, the VHX Series has completely revolutionized the instrumentation industry.

## GRFX-47C-PU: GRFX-47C-PU: 1947-53 Chevy/GMC Pickup GRFX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/grfx-47c-pu-1947-53-chevy-gmc-pickup-grfx-instruments-p1389.html` (product 1389)

### Rewrite

Bright new chrome bezels fill the two round openings in a 1947-53 Chevy or GMC truck, with GRFX graphic gauges under the lenses. Every gauge position can show an analog, digital or bar-graph readout, and you set the theme, colors and warning modes. A G-force meter shows the forces as you corner, launch and brake.

It works with anything from an old-school engine to the latest fuel injection. The sensors are in the box, each with a full-length harness. The largest screen can show a backup camera, sold separately with a full-length harness and a universal mount.

Built-in GPS calibrates the speedometer for your tire size and gearing. Bluetooth connects the Dakota Digital app for setup and live data, and the USB port takes updates. A separate control module keeps wiring tidy. It's engineered and built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, with a limited lifetime warranty.

### Original

> It doesn’t get much cleaner than the dual round openings in those 47-53 Chevy/ GMC trucks. Let the GRFX system from Dakota Digital fill the stock holes with bright, new chrome bezels surrounding the latest technology under the lenses. Compatible with an old school engine, to the latest fuel injection powerplant out there, this kit will light up your dash and keep an eye on the vitals.
>
> With GRFX you can change nearly everything: pick a graphic theme, then set colors and warning modes for each element. Each gauge position can show an analog, digital or bar-graph readout, one reading at a time, two at once, or a different reading entirely.
>
> GRFX can also show a backup camera on its largest screen, right in the cluster. Dakota Digital backup cameras are sold separately and come with a full-length harness and a universal mount.
>
> Built-in GPS calibrates the speedometer automatically, whatever your tire size or gearing. A wired speed signal keeps the reading live while GPS serves as the reference, so there's nothing to calibrate by hand. Bluetooth connects the Dakota Digital app on your phone or tablet for setup, calibration, customization and troubleshooting with live gauge and sensor data. A USB port takes updates, including new graphic themes and future features.
>
> A built-in G-force meter shows the forces at work as you corner, launch and brake.
>
> Like every Dakota Digital system, it uses a separate control module, so wiring stays tidy and less of it runs to the cluster. The sensors are included, each with a full-length harness: a water temperature probe with adapter bushings, a solid-state oil pressure sender and a speedometer pulse generator.
>
> Every Dakota Digital instrument system is engineered and built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, and carries a limited lifetime warranty.

## VFD3-39C: 1939 Chevy Digital Instrument System

Page: `redesign/shop/1939-chevy-digital-instrument-system-p99.html` (product 99)

### Rewrite

Speed, rpm, oil pressure, water temp, fuel and volts read out in bright blue or teal digits in this 1939 Chevy system. The vacuum fluorescent displays are easy to read, and the message centers add trip meters, a 0-60 timer and quarter-mile time and trap speed.

A billet aluminum dash housing made to fit the 1939 Chevy is included. A matching aluminum glove box door is sold separately, plain or with a digital clock, and the handle is separate too. The covers may need some modification. A metric version is a special order.

Water temperature, oil pressure and speed sensors are included, each with a 10 ft harness. Mount the pushbuttons where you can reach them for calibration and the message centers.

## VFD3-32F: 1932 Ford Digital Instrument System

Page: `redesign/shop/1932-ford-digital-instrument-system-p132.html` (product 132)

### Rewrite

For a 1932 Ford, this kit pairs a billet aluminum dash housing with bright vacuum fluorescent gauges in blue or teal. You can read speed, rpm, oil pressure, water temp, fuel and volts at a glance, and the message centers add trip meters, a 0-60 timer and quarter-mile times.

The billet housing is made to fit the 1932 Ford and comes in the kit. The speed sensor replaces a Ford or GM style speedometer cable and needs about 5 inches of clearance. Water temperature and oil pressure sensors are included too, each with a 10 ft harness. A metric version is a special order.

Pushbuttons you mount within reach calibrate the speedometer and fuel gauge and set the cylinder count. The same buttons page through the message centers.

## RTX-47C-PU-X: 1947-53 Chevy/ GMC Pickup RTX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1947-53-chevy-gmc-pickup-rtx-instruments-p1149.html` (product 1149)

### Rewrite

Chrome-plated billet aluminum bezels give this dual-round 1947-53 Chevy and GMC pickup cluster a decidedly stock look. The 120 mph speedometer is styled like the original, and a bar-style tachometer sits inside its sweep. A single TFT message center brings up extra data when you want more, and the LED backlighting comes on in your colors.

The machined aluminum and chrome bezels are included. Oil pressure, water temp, fuel and volts round out the readings. Water temperature, oil pressure and speed sensors come in the box, each with a 10 ft harness. A metric version is a special order.

Every RTX housing is CNC machined, and the electronics are designed and built in-house. A two-sided rocker switch handles setup, and one central control box keeps the install simple.

A single TFT message center brings up additional data for those who need just a little more!
>
> RTX keeps the stock look and adds the late-model features you want. The layout, faces and indicators follow the original design so closely that most people will take it for factory, right up until you turn the key and the LED backlighting comes on in your colors.

## HDX-73C-PU: 1973-87 Chevy Pickup, 1973-91 Chevy Blazer, GMC Jimmy and Suburbans HDX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1973-87-chevy-pickup-1973-91-chevy-blazer-gmc-jimmy-and-subu-p1040.html` (product 1040)

### Rewrite

A big speedometer and tach sit front and center in the 1973-87 Chevy and GMC pickup cluster, with the smaller gauges off to the side. This HDX system adds a TFT display in each large opening, so you can view any readings you like. Faces come in black or silver alloy, with separate lighting colors you pick.

It also fits 1973-91 Chevy Blazer, GMC Jimmy and Suburban, plus heavy duty pickups. The bezel isn't included, so use an OEM or reproduction one. A metric version is a special order.

### Original

> Still affordable and easy to find, 1973-87 Chevy and GMC pickups are gaining ground in the truck popularity contest. We love ‘em for the stock cluster arrangement; a big speedo and tach front and center with the satellites to the side. >
> HDX comes with black or silver alloy faces, and you choose separate lighting colors for the readings, needles and message centers. Set it up with the built-in touch buttons or the Bluetooth app for iPhone and Android. Needle hubs are black nickel-plated spun aluminum to match the buttons. Indicators stay hidden until they light up (turn signals, high beam, check engine, cruise control, parking brake and two you define), and every gauge has its own settable red warning light. ## HDX-67C-CAM: 1967-68 Camaro/ Firebird HDX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1967-68-camaro-firebird-hdx-instruments-p1041.html` (product 1041)

### Rewrite

Three analog gauges sit next to a full-color TFT display in each opening of the 1967-68 Camaro and Firebird dual-round cluster. Use the capacitive-touch buttons to bring just about any reading up on the two screens. Faces come in black or silver alloy.

The bezel isn't included. Use an OEM or reproduction one, and a replacement bezel is listed with this kit. A metric version is a special order.

You can also set it up with the Bluetooth app for iPhone and Android, and pick separate lighting colors for the readings, needles and message centers. Indicators stay hidden until they light, and each gauge has its own red warning light. A central control box connects the included solid-state senders, which keeps the install simple.

### Original

> Released three years after Mustang but in the lead ever since, Camaro aims to please. 1967-68 Chevy F-bodies make excellent drag cars, corner carvers, even lowriders. Any way you slice it, this instrument package makes the most of the dual-round gauge cluster. Each opening features three analog gauges snuggled up to a full-color TFT display. Summon just about any bit of information on the dual screens using the integrated, capacitive-touch buttons.
>
> HDX comes with black or silver alloy faces, and you choose separate lighting colors for the readings, needles and message centers. Set it up with the built-in touch buttons or the Bluetooth app for iPhone and Android. Needle hubs are black nickel-plated spun aluminum to match the buttons. Indicators stay hidden until they light up (turn signals, high beam, check engine, cruise control, parking brake and two you define), and every gauge has its own settable red warning light. A central control box takes signals from the vehicle harness and connects the included solid-state senders, which keeps installation simple. Full details are in the sections below.
>
> *Bezel not included; it's shown for presentation. The system uses your OEM or reproduction bezel.

## VHX-55C-PU: 1955-59 Chevy Pickup VHX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1955-59-chevy-pickup-vhx-instruments-p737.html` (product 737)

### Rewrite

Six dedicated analog instruments surround a single LCD message center in the V-shaped 1955-59 Chevy pickup cluster. The message center is big enough to show two readings at once. The needles light up, and the faces are backlit.

It fits a stock or aftermarket bezel, sold separately, and a reproduction chrome bezel for these trucks is available. It doesn't fit GMC. Faces come in black alloy, silver alloy or a carbon fiber look, in blue or red. A metric version is a special order.

Stepper motors drive the needles, and the sensors are solid state. Mount the included pushbuttons where you can reach them to calibrate the speedometer and fuel gauge. VHX is built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, with a limited lifetime warranty.

### Original

> The V-shaped cluster found in 1955-59 Chevy pickups (sorry, not GMC) is unmistakable and has found its way into more than a few custom vehicles. Capitalizing on the space, you’ll find a single LCD message center large enough to display two readings at one time, nestled in the middle of six dedicated analog instruments. Fits stock or aftermarket bezel (available separately).
>
> VHX gives you real needles that light up, backlit faces and easy-to-read LCD message centers. Underneath are micro-controlled precision stepper motors, solid-state sensors and displays you can customize. Like every Dakota Digital system, VHX is engineered and built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, and carries a limited lifetime warranty.
>
> *Bezel not included; it's shown for presentation. The system uses your OEM or reproduction bezel.

The 1959-60 Chevy dash was a home run then, and it's still popular today. This VHX system brings modern information to that classic design, with lit needles, backlit faces and easy-to-read LCD message centers.

It fits the stock instrument housings of all 1959-60 Chevy full-size cars, El Camino included, with no alterations. The system installs into the stock cast dash pods, which aren't included. Faces come in black alloy, silver alloy or a carbon fiber look, in blue or red. A metric version is a special order.

Micro-controlled stepper motors and solid-state sensors do the work behind the faces. The included pushbuttons handle calibration. Like every Dakota Digital system, VHX is built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, and has a limited lifetime warranty.

## GRFX-67C-CAM: GRFX-67C-CAM: 1967-68 Camaro/ Firebird GRFX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/grfx-67c-cam-1967-68-camaro-firebird-grfx-instruments-p1394.html` (product 1394)

### Rewrite

Bring GRFX graphic gauges to a first-gen 1967-68 Chevy Camaro or Pontiac Firebird. Each gauge position can show an analog, digital or bar-graph readout, one reading or two. Start from a graphic theme, then set colors and warning modes for each element.

The largest screen can also show a backup camera. Cameras are sold separately with a full-length harness and a universal mount. The sensors are in the box: a water temperature probe with adapter bushings, a solid-state oil pressure sender and a speedometer pulse generator.

Built-in GPS keeps the speedometer calibrated for your tires and gearing. Bluetooth connects the Dakota Digital app, and a USB port takes updates. A G-force meter shows the forces when you corner, launch and brake. A separate control module keeps wiring tidy, and the system is built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota.

### Original

> When GM introduced their pony cars in the late ‘60’s, I’ll bet they never envisioned the legacy that would be carried on for decades.. Here is the GRFX series for your first Gen, 1967-68 Chevy Camaro and Pontiac Firebird.
>
> With GRFX you can change nearly everything: pick a graphic theme, then set colors and warning modes for each element. Each gauge position can show an analog, digital or bar-graph readout, one reading at a time, two at once, or a different reading entirely.
>
> GRFX can also show a backup camera on its largest screen, right in the cluster. Dakota Digital backup cameras are sold separately and come with a full-length harness and a universal mount.
>
> Built-in GPS calibrates the speedometer automatically, whatever your tire size or gearing. A wired speed signal keeps the reading live while GPS serves as the reference, so there's nothing to calibrate by hand. Bluetooth connects the Dakota Digital app on your phone or tablet for setup, calibration, customization and troubleshooting with live gauge and sensor data. A USB port takes updates, including new graphic themes and future features.
>
> A built-in G-force meter shows the forces at work as you corner, launch and brake.
>
> Like every Dakota Digital system, it uses a separate control module, so wiring stays tidy and less of it runs to the cluster. The sensors are included, each with a full-length harness: a water temperature probe with adapter bushings, a solid-state oil pressure sender and a speedometer pulse generator.
>
> Every Dakota Digital instrument system is engineered and built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, and carries a limited lifetime warranty.

## VFD3-35F: 1935 -1936 Ford Digital Instrument System

Page: `redesign/shop/1935-1936-ford-digital-instrument-system-p134.html` (product 134)

### Rewrite

A billet aluminum dash housing and bright vacuum fluorescent gauges come together in this 1935-36 Ford kit. The Series III displays are easy to read, in blue or teal, and the message centers add trip meters and a 0-60 timer.

The housing is made to fit the 1935-36 Ford. A matching aluminum glove box cover, a handle and a clock panel with a VFD clock are sold separately, and the covers may need some modification. A metric version is a special order.

Water temperature and oil pressure sensors come with 10 ft harnesses, and the speed sensor replaces a Ford or GM style cable. Mount the included pushbuttons where you can reach them to calibrate the system and scroll the message centers.

### Original

## RTX-66C-CVL-X: 1966-67 Chevy Chevelle/ El Camino RTX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1966-67-chevy-chevelle-el-camino-rtx-instruments-p1152.html` (product 1152)

### Rewrite

A long, sweeping speedometer flanked by dual gauges keeps the factory feel of a 1966-67 Chevelle, Malibu or El Camino. Turn the key and two 1.8 inch TFT message centers, LED backlighting and hidden indicators come alive. An LED bar-graph tach sits just below the speedometer, invisible until the key is on, and the horizontal lines on the face stay for a stock look.

It uses your OEM or reproduction bezel, which isn't included. A reproduction bezel for the 1966-67 Chevelle is listed with this kit. Water temperature, oil pressure and speed sensors come with 10 ft harnesses. A metric version is a special order.

Every RTX housing is CNC machined, and the electronics are designed and built in-house. A two-sided rocker switch handles setup, and one central control box keeps installation simple.

### Original

> A long, sweeping speedometer flanked by a pair of dual gauges keeps the OEM feel of your 1966-67 Chevelle, Malibu or El Camino, but the two 1.8” TFT message centers, LED backlighting and hidden indicators bring your view straight into the future. Just below the speedometer and invisible until the key is on, you’ll find an LED bar-graph tach, plus we kept the horizontal lines on the face for the ultimate in stock appearance.
>
> RTX keeps the stock look and adds the late-model features you want. The layout, faces and indicators follow the original design so closely that most people will take it for factory, right up until you turn the key and the LED backlighting comes on in your colors.
>
> Every RTX housing is CNC machined, and the electronics are designed and built in-house. The high-definition TFT message centers show just about any reading you want, and one central control box keeps installation and setup simple. Full details are in the sections below.
>
> *Bezel not included; it's shown for presentation. The system uses your OEM or reproduction bezel.

## HDX-69C-CAM: 1969 Camaro HDX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1969-camaro-hdx-instruments-p1042.html` (product 1042)

### Rewrite

The square gauge openings of a 1969 Camaro get a full six-gauge HDX system with TFT message centers. Billet trim rings are included. The message centers show trip meters, miles to empty and 0-60 times, and you pick black or silver alloy faces with separate lighting colors for the readings, needles and message centers.

It bolts into a stock or reproduction plastic gauge housing and bezel, sold separately, and uses an OEM or reproduction instrument carrier. A 1969 Camaro carrier is listed with this kit. A metric version is a special order.

Set it up with the touch buttons or the Bluetooth app for iPhone and Android. Each gauge has its own red warning light you can set, and indicators stay hidden until they light. A central control box connects the included solid-state senders.

### Original

> Muscle cars don’t get much better than the 1969 Chevy Camaro. Its muscular flanks look good with lots of rubber stuffed underneath, while the square gauge openings inside look best with Dakota Digital instrumentation. You get a full six-gauge system, including billet trim rings and TFT-display message centers, that’s ready to bolt into a stock or reproduction plastic gauge housing/bezel (available separately).
>
> HDX comes with black or silver alloy faces, and you choose separate lighting colors for the readings, needles and message centers. Set it up with the built-in touch buttons or the Bluetooth app for iPhone and Android. Needle hubs are black nickel-plated spun aluminum to match the buttons. Indicators stay hidden until they light up (turn signals, high beam, check engine, cruise control, parking brake and two you define), and every gauge has its own settable red warning light. A central control box takes signals from the vehicle harness and connects the included solid-state senders, which keeps installation simple. Full details are in the sections below.
>
> *Gauge trim rings included. System uses OEM or reproduction style Instrument Carrier Assembly.

## VHX-68C-CVL: 1968 Chevy Chevelle/ El Camino VHX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1968-chevy-chevelle-el-camino-vhx-instruments-p747.html` (product 747)

### Rewrite

Keep the classic look of the original dual-gauge pod in a '68 Chevelle, El Camino or Malibu and get the vehicle information you want at your fingertips. VHX fills that pod with lit needles, backlit faces and LCD message centers that show trip meters, a 0-60 timer and quarter-mile times.

It's an easy retrofit with no changes to the dash. It attaches directly to an OEM or aftermarket dash bezel, and the factory bezel, trim and fascia aren't included. Faces come in black alloy, silver alloy or a carbon fiber look, in blue or red. A metric version is a special order.

Micro-controlled stepper motors and solid-state sensors sit behind the faces, and the included pushbuttons handle calibration. It's engineered and built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, with a limited lifetime warranty.

> VHX gives you real needles that light up, backlit faces and easy-to-read LCD message centers. ## GRFX-55C: GRFX-55C: 1955-56 Chevy Car GRFX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/grfx-55c-1955-56-chevy-car-grfx-instruments-p1396.html` (product 1396)

### Rewrite

For many enthusiasts, the love of cars started with the Tri-Five, and now a '55 or '56 Chevy car can run GRFX gauges. Each gauge position can show a needle, a number or a bar graph, and you choose the theme, colors and warning modes.

An optional GLC clock with a chromed, machined aluminum bezel is sold separately, and so is a replacement speaker bezel. The sensors are included with full-length harnesses. Backup cameras, which the largest screen can show, are sold separately.

GPS calibrates the speedometer for your tires and gearing. The Dakota Digital app connects over Bluetooth, a USB port takes updates, and a G-force meter tracks cornering, launch and braking. It's engineered and built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, with a limited lifetime warranty.

### Original

> The start of the “Tri-Five” era, this is where the love of cars began for many enthusiasts worldwide. Now you can have the new GRFX series of gauges for the ’55 or ’56 Chevy Car. An optional GLC Clock is also available, complete with a chromed machined aluminum bezel.
>
> With GRFX you can change nearly everything: pick a graphic theme, then set colors and warning modes for each element. Each gauge position can show an analog, digital or bar-graph readout, one reading at a time, two at once, or a different reading entirely.
>
> GRFX can also show a backup camera on its largest screen, right in the cluster. Dakota Digital backup cameras are sold separately and come with a full-length harness and a universal mount.
>
> Built-in GPS calibrates the speedometer automatically, whatever your tire size or gearing. A wired speed signal keeps the reading live while GPS serves as the reference, so there's nothing to calibrate by hand. Bluetooth connects the Dakota Digital app on your phone or tablet for setup, calibration, customization and troubleshooting with live gauge and sensor data. A USB port takes updates, including new graphic themes and future features.
>
> A built-in G-force meter shows the forces at work as you corner, launch and brake.
>
> Like every Dakota Digital system, it uses a separate control module, so wiring stays tidy and less of it runs to the cluster. The sensors are included, each with a full-length harness: a water temperature probe with adapter bushings, a solid-state oil pressure sender and a speedometer pulse generator.
>
> Every Dakota Digital instrument system is engineered and built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, and carries a limited lifetime warranty.

## RTX-67C-CAM-X: 1967-68 Camaro/ Firebird RTX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1967-68-camaro-firebird-rtx-instruments-p1154.html` (product 1154)

### Rewrite

At first look it's a simple speedometer and tach combo, styled after the stock 1967 or 1968 Camaro cluster. Turn the key and four more readings appear as LED bars for oil pressure, coolant temperature, fuel and volts. Most people won't see them, but the driver has them front and center. Each pod also has a TFT message center for extra information.

The 1967 and 1968 graphic styles differ, but both fit either year, so pick the period-correct look or the one you like. It fits directly into a stock or reproduction gauge bezel, sold separately, and a replacement bezel is listed with this kit. A metric version of each style is a special order.

Every RTX housing is CNC machined, and the electronics are designed and built in-house. A two-sided rocker switch handles setup, and one central control box keeps installation simple.

### Original

> Designed after stock 1967 and 1968 Camaro clusters, these all-new packages look like a simple speed/tach combo until you turn the key, then four auxiliary readings appear! Oil pressure, coolant temperature, fuel and volts are comprised of LED bars; invisible to the average observer but front and center for the driver. Each pod has a TFT message center for additional information at your finger tips. Although the graphic styles vary between 1967 and 1968, the systems will interchange between the years, so choose your preferred, or period-correct version! Will fit directly into stock or reproduction gauge bezels, sold separately.
>
> RTX keeps the stock look and adds the late-model features you want. The layout, faces and indicators follow the original design so closely that most people will take it for factory, right up until you turn the key and the LED backlighting comes on in your colors.
>
> Every RTX housing is CNC machined, and the electronics are designed and built in-house. The high-definition TFT message centers show just about any reading you want, and one central control box keeps installation and setup simple. Full details are in the sections below.
>
> *Bezel not included; it's shown for presentation. The system uses your OEM or reproduction bezel.

## HDX-78C-MC: 1978-88 Chevy Monte Carlo, 1978-87 Chevy El Camino/ Malibu/ Caballero HDX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1978-88-chevy-monte-carlo-1978-87-chevy-el-camino-malibu-cab-p1055.html` (product 1055)

### Rewrite

G-body owners with the six-round gauge cluster get six analog readings and a pair of TFT message centers with this HDX system. The turn signals stay in their stock locations. Faces come in black or silver alloy, with lighting colors you choose.

It fits the 1978-88 Monte Carlo and 1978-87 El Camino, Malibu and Caballero with the six-round cluster. It won't fit the rectangular sweep-style speedometer cluster. Use your stock housing, blackout plate and front bezel for an easy upgrade. The bezel isn't included. A metric version is a special order.

Set it up with the built-in touch buttons or the Bluetooth app for iPhone and Android. Every gauge has its own settable red warning light. A central control box takes signals from the car's harness and connects the included solid-state senders.

## GRFX-66C-CVL: GRFX-66C-CVL: 1966-67 Chevy Chevelle/ El Camino GRFX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/grfx-66c-cvl-1966-67-chevy-chevelle-el-camino-grfx-instrumen-p1400.html` (product 1400)

### Rewrite

Four high-resolution TFT screens fill the long sweep dash of a 1966-67 Chevelle. Each gauge position can show an analog, digital or bar-graph readout, and you pick the theme, colors and warning modes. The largest screen can also show a backup camera.

It's a direct fit for the 1966-67 Chevelle and El Camino. It uses your OEM or reproduction bezel, which isn't included, and a replacement bezel is listed with this kit. Backup cameras are sold separately. The sensors come with full-length harnesses.

Built-in GPS calibrates the speedometer for your tires and gearing. The Dakota Digital app connects over Bluetooth, and a USB port takes updates. A G-force meter shows the forces when you corner, launch and brake. It's engineered and built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, with a limited lifetime warranty.

### Original

> Fill up that long sweep dash in your A-body with these 4 Hi-Res TFT screens in the all new direct fit GRFX series for 1966- 67 Chevelle’s.
>
> With GRFX you can change nearly everything: pick a graphic theme, then set colors and warning modes for each element. Each gauge position can show an analog, digital or bar-graph readout, one reading at a time, two at once, or a different reading entirely.
>
> GRFX can also show a backup camera on its largest screen, right in the cluster. Dakota Digital backup cameras are sold separately and come with a full-length harness and a universal mount.
>
> Built-in GPS calibrates the speedometer automatically, whatever your tire size or gearing. A wired speed signal keeps the reading live while GPS serves as the reference, so there's nothing to calibrate by hand. Bluetooth connects the Dakota Digital app on your phone or tablet for setup, calibration, customization and troubleshooting with live gauge and sensor data. A USB port takes updates, including new graphic themes and future features.
>
> A built-in G-force meter shows the forces at work as you corner, launch and brake.
>
> Like every Dakota Digital system, it uses a separate control module, so wiring stays tidy and less of it runs to the cluster. The sensors are included, each with a full-length harness: a water temperature probe with adapter bushings, a solid-state oil pressure sender and a speedometer pulse generator.
>
> Every Dakota Digital instrument system is engineered and built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, and carries a limited lifetime warranty.
>
> *Bezel not included; it's shown for presentation. The system uses your OEM or reproduction bezel.

## RTX-66F-BRO-X: 1966-77 Ford Bronco RTX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1966-77-ford-bronco-rtx-instruments-p1175.html` (product 1175)

### Rewrite

Orange needles and spun-aluminum accents on the face give this 1966-77 Bronco cluster a factory look, and it adds a tachometer. A large, full-color TFT message center shows the readings you choose. A casual glance says original equipment, until the LED backlighting comes on in your color scheme.

It fits your stock steel instrument bezel, which isn't included. It also fits 1961-66 Ford pickups with the stock bezel, except Custom Cab. A metric version in km/h and Celsius is a special order.

Every RTX housing is CNC machined, and the electronics are designed and built in-house. Water temperature, oil pressure and speed sensors come with 10 ft harnesses. A two-sided rocker switch handles setup, and one central control box keeps installation simple.

### Original

Also fits 1961-66 Ford pickup (except Custom Cab) with stock bezel. The RTX series is the latest from Dakota Digital, focused on retaining that stock look without sacrificing the late-model features you love. Every effort has been put forth to incorporate OEM design elements regarding the layout, face styling and indicators. A casual glance may dismiss these as original equipment, but once you hit the switch and the LED backlighting comes on in your favorite color scheme, all doubts will be removed.

## HDX-64C-CVL: 1964-65 Chevy Chevelle/ Malibu/ El Camino HDX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1964-65-chevy-chevelle-malibu-el-camino-hdx-instruments-p1058.html` (product 1058)

### Rewrite

Six analog gauge sweeps and a pair of high-visibility TFT message centers bring a 1964-65 Chevelle, Malibu or El Camino up to spec. This direct-fit HDX system comes with black or silver faces, and you choose separate lighting colors for the readings, needles and message centers.

It attaches directly to a stock or reproduction bezel, sold separately. Reproduction bezels are listed with this kit for 1964 and 1965 cars, with or without A/C. A metric version is a special order.

Set it up with the touch buttons or the Bluetooth app for iPhone and Android. Indicators stay hidden until they light, and every gauge has its own red warning light you can set. A central control box connects the included solid-state senders, which keeps installation simple.

### Original

> Ushering in a new era of muscle machines, the 1964-65 Chevy A bodies have long been turned into hot rods. Keep the tradition alive and bring yours up to spec with this direct-fit instrument system! It attaches directly to a stock or reproduction bezel (available separately) and supplies you with six analog gauge sweeps and a pair of high-visibility TFT message centers. Black or silver face styling is available.
>
> HDX comes with black or silver alloy faces, and you choose separate lighting colors for the readings, needles and message centers. Set it up with the built-in touch buttons or the Bluetooth app for iPhone and Android. Needle hubs are black nickel-plated spun aluminum to match the buttons. Indicators stay hidden until they light up (turn signals, high beam, check engine, cruise control, parking brake and two you define), and every gauge has its own settable red warning light. A central control box takes signals from the vehicle harness and connects the included solid-state senders, which keeps installation simple. Full details are in the sections below.
>
> *Bezel not included; it's shown for presentation. The system uses your OEM or reproduction bezel.

Any 1970-81 Camaro can bolt in this full-function VHX system and keep its classic look. Real needles light up over backlit faces, and LCD message centers show trip meters, a 0-60 timer and quarter-mile times.

## GRFX-70C-CAM: GRFX-70C-CAM: 1970-81 Chevy Camaro GRFX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/grfx-70c-cam-1970-81-chevy-camaro-grfx-instruments-p1435.html` (product 1435)

### Rewrite

The 1970-81 Camaro's European-inspired cockpit was full of driver-focused gauges, and GRFX swaps them for full-digital TFT displays. You can customize them down to the last detail, from graphic themes and display colors to how each gauge position shows its reading.

It bolts right in. The bezel isn't included. An optional backup camera, sold separately, shows right in the cluster. The sensors are included, each with a full-length harness.

Set it up over Bluetooth with the Dakota Digital app. Built-in GPS calibrates the speedometer for your tires and gearing, a USB port takes updates, and a G-force meter shows cornering, launch and braking forces. It's engineered and built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, with a limited lifetime warranty.

### Original

> The European-inspired design of 1970-81 Camaros gave us a cockpit full of driver-oriented instruments. They were cutting-edge in 1970 but are outdated by today’s standards. This GRFX package bolts right in and replaces the stock gauges with full-digital TFT displays you can customize down to the last detail. Choose from multiple graphic themes and display colors, set up the system over Bluetooth with the Dakota Digital app, and add an optional backup camera that shows right in the cluster.
>
> **Bezel not included**
>
> With GRFX you can change nearly everything: pick a graphic theme, then set colors and warning modes for each element. Each gauge position can show an analog, digital or bar-graph readout, one reading at a time, two at once, or a different reading entirely.
>
> GRFX can also show a backup camera on its largest screen, right in the cluster. Dakota Digital backup cameras are sold separately and come with a full-length harness and a universal mount.
>
> Built-in GPS calibrates the speedometer automatically, whatever your tire size or gearing. A wired speed signal keeps the reading live while GPS serves as the reference, so there's nothing to calibrate by hand. Bluetooth connects the Dakota Digital app on your phone or tablet for setup, calibration, customization and troubleshooting with live gauge and sensor data. A USB port takes updates, including new graphic themes and future features.
>
> A built-in G-force meter shows the forces at work as you corner, launch and brake.
>
> Like every Dakota Digital system, it uses a separate control module, so wiring stays tidy and less of it runs to the cluster. The sensors are included, each with a full-length harness: a water temperature probe with adapter bushings, a solid-state oil pressure sender and a speedometer pulse generator.
>
> Every Dakota Digital instrument system is engineered and built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, and carries a limited lifetime warranty.
>
> **Bezel not included**

# Batch 8

Each product: the rewrite, the original description as it was on the page, and every fact in the rewrite with where it came from. Sources: Description (the main copy), Description footnote (the * notes), Specifications / Extra Items / Pictures tabs, Options and Versions in the buy box, and the product title. ## RTX-60C-PU-X: 1960-63 Chevy Pickup RTX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1960-63-chevy-pickup-rtx-instruments-p1185.html` (product 1185)

### Rewrite

Keep the vintage look of your 1960-63 Chevy C10 and get modern readouts. A bar-graph tachometer is tucked under the 120 mph speedometer, the turn signals keep their factory style, and a full-color TFT message center sits in the middle. The LED backlighting comes on in your colors when you turn the key.

Drop it into a stock or reproduction bezel, which isn't included. It doesn't fit GMC. A metric version is a special order.

Every RTX housing is CNC machined, and the electronics are designed and built in-house. Water temperature, oil pressure and speed sensors come with 10 ft harnesses. A two-sided rocker switch handles setup, and one central control box keeps installation simple.

## HDX-68C-CVL: 1968 Chevy Chevelle/ El Camino HDX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1968-chevy-chevelle-el-camino-hdx-instruments-p1060.html` (product 1060)

### Rewrite

Those two large squares in the 1968 Chevelle, Malibu and El Camino dash have long been a puzzle. This HDX system bolts in and fills them with six analog gauge readings and a pair of TFT message centers. Pick black or silver alloy faces and your own lighting colors.

It uses your OEM or reproduction bezel, which isn't included. A metric version is a special order.

Set it up with the built-in touch buttons or the Bluetooth app for iPhone and Android. Indicators stay hidden until they light, and every gauge has its own settable red warning light. A central control box takes signals from the car's harness and connects the included solid-state senders, which keeps installation simple.

### Original

> What can you do with a pair of large squares in your dash? This has been a puzzling question for 1968 Chevelle, Malibu and El Camino pilots, but now the answer is clear! Bolt in this HDX instrument system and you’ll be endowed with six analog gauge readings as well as a pair of TFT digital message centers.
>
> HDX comes with black or silver alloy faces, and you choose separate lighting colors for the readings, needles and message centers. Set it up with the built-in touch buttons or the Bluetooth app for iPhone and Android. Needle hubs are black nickel-plated spun aluminum to match the buttons. Indicators stay hidden until they light up (turn signals, high beam, check engine, cruise control, parking brake and two you define), and every gauge has its own settable red warning light. A central control box takes signals from the vehicle harness and connects the included solid-state senders, which keeps installation simple. Full details are in the sections below.
>
> *Bezel not included; it's shown for presentation. The system uses your OEM or reproduction bezel.
