## VHX-61F-PU: 1961-66 Ford Pickup VHX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1961-66-ford-pickup-vhx-instruments-p779.html` (product 779)

### Rewrite

The big round speedometer in a 1961-66 Ford pickup leaves out a lot of vital signs. This all-in-one VHX system packs six analog readings and two LCD message centers into the stock gauge bezel, with lit needles and backlit faces.

It doesn't fit the Bronco or the 1965-66 Custom Cab. The bezel, trim and fascia aren't included, so use OEM or reproduction parts. Faces come in black or silver alloy, and you can order blue, red or white. It works with nearly any engine and transmission combo. A metric version is a special order.

Stepper motors move the needles, and the sensors are solid state. The included pushbuttons calibrate the speedometer and fuel gauge. VHX is engineered and built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, with a limited lifetime warranty.

### Original

> Ford pickups got a major facelift in 1961, and that smooth styling lasted all the way ‘til 1966. The big, round speedometer lacks many vital signs desired by enthusiasts, so we whipped up an all-in-one system that packs six analog readings plus two LCD message centers into the stock gauge bezel. Does not fit Bronco or ('65-66) Custom Cab models, the VHX-61F-PU can be had with Black or Silver Alloy face styling and will interface with nearly any engine/transmission combo!
>
> VHX gives you real needles that light up, backlit faces and easy-to-read LCD message centers. Underneath are micro-controlled precision stepper motors, solid-state sensors and displays you can customize. Like every Dakota Digital system, VHX is engineered and built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, and carries a limited lifetime warranty.
>
> *Bezel, trim and fascia not included; they're shown for presentation. The system uses your OEM or reproduction bezel.

## GRFX-3100: GRFX-3100: Universal Competition, Digital GRFX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/grfx-3100-universal-competition-digital-grfx-instruments-p1403.html` (product 1403)

### Rewrite

Competition-style looks in a compact package: the GRFX-3100 packs a lot of information into its TFT screen. Each gauge position can show an analog, digital or bar-graph readout, and you pick the theme, colors and warning modes.

It measures 10.75 by 6 inches overall and needs a 10.25 by 5.5 inch cutout, plus two inches of clearance behind it. The sensors are included with full-length harnesses. Backup cameras, which the screen can show, are sold separately.

A built-in rotary switch keeps installation simple. GPS calibrates the speedometer, Bluetooth links the Dakota Digital app, and a USB port takes updates. It's engineered and built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, with a limited lifetime warranty.

### Original

> Looking for the latest competition styled gauges from Dakota Digital? Check out the GRFX-3100. This compact unit measures 10.75" x 6" (10.25" x 5-1/2" cutout) overall and is packed full of information in the TFT screen display, requiring two inches of clearance on the backside. Built-in rotary switch for streamlined installation.
>
> With GRFX you can change nearly everything: pick a graphic theme, then set colors and warning modes for each element. Each gauge position can show an analog, digital or bar-graph readout, one reading at a time, two at once, or a different reading entirely.
>
> GRFX can also show a backup camera on its largest screen, right in the cluster. Dakota Digital backup cameras are sold separately and come with a full-length harness and a universal mount.
>
> Built-in GPS calibrates the speedometer automatically, whatever your tire size or gearing. A wired speed signal keeps the reading live while GPS serves as the reference, so there's nothing to calibrate by hand. Bluetooth connects the Dakota Digital app on your phone or tablet for setup, calibration, customization and troubleshooting with live gauge and sensor data. A USB port takes updates, including new graphic themes and future features.
>
> A built-in G-force meter shows the forces at work as you corner, launch and brake.
>
> Like every Dakota Digital system, it uses a separate control module, so wiring stays tidy and less of it runs to the cluster. The sensors are included, each with a full-length harness: a water temperature probe with adapter bushings, a solid-state oil pressure sender and a speedometer pulse generator.
>
> Every Dakota Digital instrument system is engineered and built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, and carries a limited lifetime warranty.

Easy-to-read vacuum fluorescent digits light up a billet aluminum dash housing made for the 1935-37 Ford pickup. You can choose blue or teal, and the readouts cover speed, rpm, oil pressure, water temp, fuel and volts. The message centers add trip meters, a 0-60 timer and quarter-mile times.

The housing comes in the kit. A metric version is a special order.

## VFD3-48F-PU: 1948 -1950 Ford F1 Pickup Digital Instrument System

Page: `redesign/shop/1948-1950-ford-f1-pickup-digital-instrument-system-p144.html` (product 144)

### Rewrite

In a 1948-50 Ford F1, this Series III system puts bright vacuum fluorescent gauges in a billet aluminum dash housing. Speed, rpm, oil pressure, water temp, fuel and volts read out in blue or teal, and the message centers add trip meters, a 0-60 timer and quarter-mile times.

The housing is made to fit the 1948-50 Ford pickup and comes in the kit. A metric version is a special order.

Water temperature, oil pressure and speed sensors come with 10 ft harnesses, and the speed sensor works with Ford or GM style cables. Mount the included pushbuttons where you can reach them. They calibrate the speedometer and fuel gauge and then scroll through the message centers.

### Original

> Series III systems use bright vacuum fluorescent displays: easy to read, accurate, and built for years of trouble-free driving.
>
> Full 5 Gauge Instrument System includes Billet Aluminum dash housing to fit 1948- 50 Ford Pickup.

## RTX-59C-IMP-X: 1959-60 Chevy Impala/ El Camino RTX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1959-60-chevy-impala-el-camino-rtx-instruments-p1189.html` (product 1189)

### Rewrite

The five-pod 1959-60 Chevy dash has become an icon, showing up in everything from kustoms to mini trucks. This RTX system keeps factory-styled faces and adds a large, full-color TFT display. You choose your own lighting colors, and a bar-graph tachometer joins the speedometer, oil, water, fuel and volt readings.

It fits the 1959-60 Impala and El Camino. You'll need the stock gauge pods, which Dakota Digital doesn't sell, and your OEM or reproduction bezel, which isn't included. A metric version is a special order.

Every RTX housing is CNC machined, and the electronics are designed and built in-house. One central control box keeps installation and setup simple.

### Original

> Fins and style for miles, but the 1959-60 Chevy dash has become an icon in the custom world. We find these quintuple clusters installed in everything from kustoms to mini trucks, and the RTX series is sure to wow them all. OE-styling for the faces with a large, full color TFT digital display and the flexibility to choose your own lighting colors make this a slam dunk. Requires use of stock gauge pods, which Dakota Digital does not offer.
>
> RTX keeps the stock look and adds the late-model features you want. The layout, faces and indicators follow the original design so closely that most people will take it for factory, right up until you turn the key and the LED backlighting comes on in your colors.
>
> Every RTX housing is CNC machined, and the electronics are designed and built in-house. The high-definition TFT message centers show just about any reading you want, and one central control box keeps installation and setup simple. Full details are in the sections below.
>
> *Bezel not included; it's shown for presentation. The system uses your OEM or reproduction bezel.

## HDX-62T-FJ: 1962-84 Toyota FJ HDX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1962-84-toyota-fj-hdx-instruments-p1062.html` (product 1062)

### Rewrite

Made for the stock gauge bezel in 1962-84 Toyota FJ models, this HDX system drops right in. Analog needles on black or silver alloy faces sit with a TFT message center, and you pick separate lighting colors for the readings and needles.

It works with nearly any drivetrain. The bezel isn't included, so use an OEM or reproduction one. A custom 4,000 rpm tach for diesels and metric units are available on request, so contact Dakota Digital about those.

Set it up with the built-in touch buttons or the Bluetooth app for iPhone and Android. Every gauge has its own settable red warning light. A central control box takes signals from the vehicle harness and connects the included solid-state senders.

### Original

> A drop-in instrument system designed for a stock gauge bezel from 1962-84 Toyota FJ models, this kit can be used with nearly any drivetrain. Custom 4,000 rpm tachometer for diesel applications and/or metric units are available upon request (please contact Dakota Digital concerning this option).
>
> HDX comes with black or silver alloy faces, and you choose separate lighting colors for the readings, needles and message centers. Set it up with the built-in touch buttons or the Bluetooth app for iPhone and Android. Needle hubs are black nickel-plated spun aluminum to match the buttons. Indicators stay hidden until they light up (turn signals, high beam, check engine, cruise control, parking brake and two you define), and every gauge has its own settable red warning light. A central control box takes signals from the vehicle harness and connects the included solid-state senders, which keeps installation simple. Full details are in the sections below.
>
> *Bezel not included; it's shown for presentation. The system uses your OEM or reproduction bezel.

## VHX-82C-CAM: 1982-89 Chevy Camaro VHX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1982-89-chevy-camaro-vhx-instruments-p781.html` (product 781)

### Rewrite

Five analog instruments and dual LCD message centers bolt into the 1982-89 Camaro with this VHX system. The voltmeter shows in the message center. Fully lit needles and backlit faces make everything easy to see.

It's a simple bolt-in, but it doesn't fit the Firebird or the Berlinetta digital dash. It uses your factory bezel, trim and fascia, which aren't included. Faces come in black alloy, silver alloy or a carbon fiber look, in blue or red. A metric version is a special order.

Micro-controlled stepper motors move the needles, and the sensors are solid state. Water temperature, oil pressure and speed sensors come with 10 ft harnesses, and the included pushbuttons handle calibration. It's engineered and built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, with a limited lifetime warranty.

### Original

>
> Fully lit needles, backlit faces, and highly visible LCD message centers are a few of the things that you can see which set the VHX Series apart from yesterday’s traditional approach to instrumentation. A few of the features which you don’t normally see are the micro-controlled precision stepper motors, solid state sensors for unparalleled accuracy, and user-customizable display feedback, all supported by an unrivaled limited lifetime warranty which has become the standard for Dakota Digital instrumentation systems . Completely engineered and manufactured at our facility in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, the VHX Series has completely revolutionized the instrumentation industry! Does not fit Berlinetta digital dash.

## RTX-63C-IMP-X: 1963-64 Chevy Impala RTX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1963-64-chevy-impala-rtx-instruments-p1199.html` (product 1199)

### Rewrite

Looks like 1963, works like today. This RTX system is drawn from the factory cluster in the 1963-64 Chevy Impala, Bel Air and Biscayne, then adds LED backlighting, TFT message centers and full color-changing lighting. Pick the 1963 or 1964 style.

Use your stock steel front bezel, which isn't supplied. Cars with factory A/C need some changes to the ductwork on the stock gauge bezel. It connects to anything from a 283 to a twin turbo LS3. A metric version of each style is a special order.

Every RTX housing is CNC machined, and the electronics are designed and built in-house. Water temperature, oil pressure and speed sensors come with 10 ft harnesses. A two-sided rocker switch handles setup, and one central control box keeps installation simple.

>
> RTX keeps the stock look and adds the late-model features you want. The layout, faces and indicators follow the original design so closely that most people will take it for factory, right up until you turn the key and the LED backlighting comes on in your colors.

## HDX-67F-MUS: 1967-68 Ford Mustang HDX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1967-68-ford-mustang-hdx-instruments-p1066.html` (product 1066)

### Rewrite

Five analog gauges and twin TFT message centers bolt straight into a 1967-68 Mustang bezel with this HDX system. Faces come in black or silver alloy, and you choose separate lighting colors for the readings, needles and message centers.

It fits a stock or reproduction 1967-68 Mustang bezel, which isn't included. A metric version is a special order.

Water temperature, oil pressure and speed sensors come with 10 ft harnesses, and the speed sensor replaces a Ford or GM style cable. Set it up with the built-in touch buttons or the Bluetooth app for iPhone and Android. Every gauge has its own red warning light you can set. A central control box connects the included senders, which keeps installation simple.

### Original

> Lieutenant Bullitt would have liked the feature list of the HDX series, keeping tabs on his 390s vitals while slinging it through San Francisco. This five-gauge analog system bolts directly into a stock or reproduction 1967-68 Mustang bezel and features twin TFT message centers. Your choice of black or silver face styling is available, so what are you waiting for?
>
> HDX comes with black or silver alloy faces, and you choose separate lighting colors for the readings, needles and message centers. Set it up with the built-in touch buttons or the Bluetooth app for iPhone and Android. Needle hubs are black nickel-plated spun aluminum to match the buttons. Indicators stay hidden until they light up (turn signals, high beam, check engine, cruise control, parking brake and two you define), and every gauge has its own settable red warning light. A central control box takes signals from the vehicle harness and connects the included solid-state senders, which keeps installation simple. Full details are in the sections below.
>
> *Bezel not included; it's shown for presentation. The system uses your OEM or reproduction bezel.

It fits a stock or reproduction dash bezel with no issues. The bezel isn't included, and reproduction bezels for 1964 and 1965 cars, with or without A/C, are listed with this kit. Faces come in black alloy, silver alloy or a carbon fiber look, in blue or red. A metric version is a special order.

It uses your OEM or reproduction bezel, which isn't included. A metric version is a special order.

Water temperature, oil pressure and speed sensors come in the box, each with a 10 ft harness. The speed sensor replaces a Ford or GM style cable and needs about 5 inches of clearance. Pushbuttons you mount within reach calibrate the speedometer and fuel gauge, set the cylinder count and page through the message centers.

### Original

> Series III systems use bright vacuum fluorescent displays: easy to read, accurate, and built for years of trouble-free driving.
>
> Full 6 Gauge Instrument System fits into OEM bezel for 1966- 77 Ford Bronco.
>
> *Bezel not included; it's shown for presentation. The system uses your OEM or reproduction bezel.

## RTX-66F-BRO-X-p1187: 1961-66 Ford Pickup RTX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1961-66-ford-pickup-rtx-instruments-p1187.html` (product 1187)

### Rewrite

People will look twice at the dash of your 1961-66 F100. This factory look-alike keeps the vintage style, adds a tachometer and puts a large, full-color TFT message center in the middle that shows the readings you choose. Orange needles and spun-aluminum accents on the face finish the look, and the LED backlighting comes on in your color scheme.

It fits your existing bezel and needs a stock steel bezel, which isn't included. It doesn't fit Custom Cab models. A metric version is a special order.

Every RTX housing is CNC machined, and the electronics are designed and built in-house. Water temperature, oil pressure and speed sensors come with 10 ft harnesses. A two-sided rocker switch handles setup, and one central control box keeps installation simple.

### Original

> Make ‘em do a double-take when they peer inside your 1961-66 F100. This factory look-alike fits into your existing bezel and delivers a vintage look while adding a tachometer and host of high-tech features. The large, full-color TFT message center can be customized with your desired readings, and the spun-aluminum accents on the instrument face and orange needles bring the look home. Does not fit Custom Cab models and does require a stock steel bezel. The RTX series is the latest from Dakota Digital, focused on retaining that stock look without sacrificing the late-model features you love. Every effort has been put forth to incorporate OEM design elements regarding the layout, face styling and indicators. A casual glance may dismiss these as original equipment, but once you hit the switch and the LED backlighting comes on in your favorite color scheme, all doubts will be removed.
>
> Every RTX housing is CNC machined, and the electronics are designed and built in-house. The high-definition TFT message centers show just about any reading you want, and one central control box keeps installation and setup simple. Full details are in the sections below.
>
> *Bezel not included; it's shown for presentation. The system uses your OEM bezel.

## HDX-66F-BRO-p1064: 1961-66 Ford Pickup HDX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1961-66-ford-pickup-hdx-instruments-p1064.html` (product 1064)

### Rewrite

Here's an all-in-one analog system with a large TFT message center, made to fit the stock dash of a 1961-66 Ford pickup. Choose black or silver alloy faces and set separate lighting colors for the readings and needles.

It uses your existing bezel, which isn't included, and it doesn't fit Custom Cab models. It connects to just about any drivetrain combination. A metric version is a special order.

Set it up with the built-in touch buttons or the Bluetooth app for iPhone and Android. Every gauge has its own settable red warning light. Water temperature, oil pressure and speed sensors come with 10 ft harnesses, and a central control box keeps the wiring simple.

### Original

> Here’s the answer for your 1961-66 Ford pickup; an all-in-one analog instrument system with a large TFT message center, designed to fit your stock dash. This systems uses your existing bezel, and does not fit Custom Cab models, but man it looks good! Choose from black or silver face styling, and feel free to connect to just about any drivetrain combination.
>
> HDX comes with black or silver alloy faces, and you choose separate lighting colors for the readings, needles and message centers. Set it up with the built-in touch buttons or the Bluetooth app for iPhone and Android. Needle hubs are black nickel-plated spun aluminum to match the buttons. Indicators stay hidden until they light up (turn signals, high beam, check engine, cruise control, parking brake and two you define), and every gauge has its own settable red warning light. A central control box takes signals from the vehicle harness and connects the included solid-state senders, which keeps installation simple. Full details are in the sections below.
>
> *Bezel not included; it's shown for presentation. The system uses your OEM or reproduction bezel.

## VHX-35F-p874: 1935 -36 Ford Car VHX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1935-36-ford-car-vhx-instruments-p874.html` (product 874)

### Rewrite

The 1935-36 Ford often gets overshadowed, but it looks great as a street rod or a kustom. This direct-fit VHX system fills its dash with chrome and modern readouts while keeping a classic look. Lit needles sit over backlit faces, and LCD message centers add trip meters, a 0-60 timer and quarter-mile times.

A new chrome bezel is included. It measures 11.38 by 4.63 inches overall and needs an 11 by 4.19 inch cutout. It also fits the 1939 Ford car. Faces come in black or silver alloy, and you can order blue, red or white. A metric version is a special order.

Stepper motors move the needles, and the included pushbuttons handle calibration. VHX is built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, with a limited lifetime warranty.

### Original

> Despite looking good as a street rod or a kustom, the 1935 and ’36 Ford are often overshadowed by their more popular siblings; take advantage of the diversity and outfit your Ford with this direct-fit instrument system. Oozing with chrome and high-tech abilities with a classic look, this setup will make people wonder why there aren’t more of these cars at the show. Also fits 1939 Ford car.
>
> VHX gives you real needles that light up, backlit faces and easy-to-read LCD message centers. Underneath are micro-controlled precision stepper motors, solid-state sensors and displays you can customize. Like every Dakota Digital system, VHX is engineered and built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, and carries a limited lifetime warranty.
>
> Dimensions: Overall: 11.38" W x 4.63" H Cutout: 11" W x 4.19" H
>
> *Includes new chrome bezel shown

Give a 1994-95 Impala SS six digital gauges on bright vacuum fluorescent displays. Speed, rpm, oil pressure, water temp, fuel and volts read out in blue or teal, and the message centers add trip meters, a 0-60 timer and quarter-mile times.

The system fits your existing OEM bezel and uses the OEM mounting hardware, and neither comes with it. A metric version is a special order.

Water temperature, oil pressure and speed sensors are in the box, each with a 10 ft harness. Mount the included pushbuttons within reach for calibration and the message centers.

### Original

> Series III systems use bright vacuum fluorescent displays: easy to read, accurate, and built for years of trouble-free driving.
>
> Full 6 Gauge Instrusment System fits into OEM bezel and uses OEM mounting hardware fo 1994- 95 Chevy Impala SS .
>
> Fits directly into existing bezel and mounting hardware (not included).

## HDX-59C-IMP: 1959-60 Chevy Impala/ El Camino HDX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1959-60-chevy-impala-el-camino-hdx-instruments-p1068.html` (product 1068)

### Rewrite

Whatever your 1959-60 Chevy cast pods ended up in, this HDX package installs directly into the stock housings. You get six analog sweep readouts and a large, full-color TFT message center. Choose black or silver alloy faces and your own lighting colors.

It fits the 1959-60 Impala and El Camino. The bezel isn't included, so use an OEM or reproduction one. A metric version is a special order.

Set it up with the built-in touch buttons or the Bluetooth app for iPhone and Android. Indicators stay hidden until they light, and every gauge has its own red warning light you can set. A central control box takes signals from the car's harness and connects the included solid-state senders.

### Original

> There may be as many 1959-60 Chevy dashes in custom vehicles as originally installed by GM. Whatever your original cast pods are in, this package is designed to install directly into stock housings, and brings six analog sweep readouts plus a large, full color TFT message center. Choose from black or silver face styling, and your sled is ready for the next century!
>
> HDX comes with black or silver alloy faces, and you choose separate lighting colors for the readings, needles and message centers. Set it up with the built-in touch buttons or the Bluetooth app for iPhone and Android. Needle hubs are black nickel-plated spun aluminum to match the buttons. Indicators stay hidden until they light up (turn signals, high beam, check engine, cruise control, parking brake and two you define), and every gauge has its own settable red warning light. A central control box takes signals from the vehicle harness and connects the included solid-state senders, which keeps installation simple. Full details are in the sections below.
>
> *Bezel not included; it's shown for presentation. The system uses your OEM or reproduction bezel.

# Batch 12

Each product: the rewrite, the original description as it was on the page, and every fact in the rewrite with where it came from. ## RTX-32F-X: 1932 Ford Car RTX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1932-ford-car-rtx-instruments-p1210.html` (product 1210)

### Rewrite

Your deuce gets a factory-styled cluster with a full-color TFT display built in. The speedometer and fuel gauge are analog, while the tach, oil pressure and water temp show as LED bar graphs. Voltage reads in the message center. It looks like original equipment until the LED backlighting comes on in your color scheme.

It's a direct fit for the 1932 Ford car, and the machined aluminum bezel fits the stock dash. It measures 12.135 by 4.925 inches overall and needs an 11.836 by 4.626 inch cutout. A metric version is a special order.

Every RTX housing is CNC machined, and the electronics are designed and built in-house. Water temperature, oil pressure and speed sensors come with 10 ft harnesses, and the speed sensor works with Ford or GM style cables. A two-sided rocker switch handles setup.

### Original

> The quintessential hot rod needs the best when it comes to instrumentation, and this direct-fit masterpiece is it! Combining the stock look with thoroughly modern technology, there is no need look elsewhere when planning your deuce. A full-color TFT display meshes seamlessly with the OE-styling, and the machined aluminum bezel fits the stock dash without incident. The RTX series is the latest from Dakota Digital, focused on retaining that stock look without sacrificing the late-model features you love. Every effort has been put forth to incorporate OEM design elements regarding the layout, face styling and indicators. A casual glance may dismiss these as original equipment, but once you hit the switch and the LED backlighting comes on in your favorite color scheme, all doubts will be removed.
>
> Every RTX housing is CNC machined, and the electronics are designed and built in-house. The high-definition TFT message centers show just about any reading you want, and one central control box keeps installation and setup simple. Full details are in the sections below.
>
> Dimensions: Overall: 12.135" W x 4.925" H Cutout: 11.836" W x 4.626" H

This bolt-in RTX system adds a tach on the right, with fuel and temp gauges beside the speedometer. The fully machined aluminum housing is powdercoated black. It uses your OEM or reproduction bezel, which isn't included. A metric version is a special order.

Every RTX housing is CNC machined, and the electronics are designed and built in-house. Water temperature, oil pressure and speed sensors come with 10 ft harnesses. A two-sided rocker switch handles setup, and one central control box keeps installation simple.

New reproduction chrome bezels come with this six-gauge system for 1947-53 Chevy and GMC pickups. The Series III vacuum fluorescent displays are bright and easy to read, in blue or teal. Message centers add trip meters, a 0-60 timer and quarter-mile times.

The bezels are made to fit the 1947-53 trucks. A metric version is a special order.

Water temperature, oil pressure and speed sensors are included, each with a 10 ft harness, and the speed sensor needs about 5 inches of clearance. Mount the included pushbuttons where you can reach them. They calibrate the speedometer and fuel gauge and then scroll through the message centers.

### Original

> Series III systems use bright vacuum fluorescent displays: easy to read, accurate, and built for years of trouble-free driving.
>
> Full 6 Gauge Instrument System includes new reproduction chrome bezels to fit 1947- 53 Chevy/ GMC Pickup.

# Batch 13

Each product: the rewrite, the original description as it was on the page, and every fact in the rewrite with where it came from. Sources: Description (the main copy), Description footnote (the * notes), Specifications / Extra Items / Pictures tabs, Options and Versions in the buy box, and the product title. ## RTX-69F-MUS-X: 1969-70 Ford Mustang RTX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1969-70-ford-mustang-rtx-instruments-p1221.html` (product 1221)

### Rewrite

Bolted behind the stock bezel of a '69-70 Mustang, this RTX system keeps the OEM styling and vintage feel. Full LED backlighting makes the gauges easy to see at night, and TFT message centers keep you informed of what's happening under the hood.

It's a direct fit that uses your OEM or reproduction bezel, which isn't included. Add the matching RLC clock for the passenger side, sold separately. A metric version is a special order.

Every RTX housing is CNC machined, and the electronics are designed and built in-house. Water temperature, oil pressure and speed sensors come with 10 ft harnesses, and the speed sensor works with Ford or GM style cables. A two-sided rocker switch handles setup.

### Original

> With this direct-fit package bolted behind the stock bezel, driving your ’69-70 Mustang will never be the same. Full LED backlighting makes night visibility second to none, and the OEM styling will keep the vintage vibe you’re going for. The TFT message centers will keep you informed of underhood happenings, and you can add the RLC clock to the passenger side for a complete upgrade.
>
> RTX keeps the stock look and adds the late-model features you want. The layout, faces and indicators follow the original design so closely that most people will take it for factory, right up until you turn the key and the LED backlighting comes on in your colors.
>
> Every RTX housing is CNC machined, and the electronics are designed and built in-house. The high-definition TFT message centers show just about any reading you want, and one central control box keeps installation and setup simple. Full details are in the sections below.
>
> *Bezel not included; it's shown for presentation. The system uses your OEM or reproduction bezel.

## HDX-63C-NOV: 1963-65 Chevy Nova HDX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1963-65-chevy-nova-hdx-instruments-p1071.html` (product 1071)

### Rewrite

The small instrument housing of a 1963-65 Chevy II doesn't leave much room, but this HDX system fits six analog readings and a full-color TFT for extra information. Pick black or silver alloy faces and your own lighting colors.

Use your stock or reproduction bezel, which isn't included. It also works in a 1962 car if you add a 1963-65 bezel. A metric version is a special order.

Water temperature, oil pressure and speed sensors come with 10 ft harnesses, and the speed sensor needs about 5 inches of clearance. Set it up with the touch buttons or the Bluetooth app for iPhone and Android. A central control box connects the senders, which keeps installation simple.

### Original

> Chevy’s first economy car is now a favorite for speed freaks of all kinds. The small instrument housing of the 1963-65 Chevy II isn’t much to work with, but the HDX instrument system packs six analog readings with analog readouts, plus a full color TFT for additional information. Use your stock or reproduction bezel; can be used in 1962 models with the addition of 1963-65 bezel.
>
> HDX comes with black or silver alloy faces, and you choose separate lighting colors for the readings, needles and message centers. Set it up with the built-in touch buttons or the Bluetooth app for iPhone and Android. Needle hubs are black nickel-plated spun aluminum to match the buttons. Indicators stay hidden until they light up (turn signals, high beam, check engine, cruise control, parking brake and two you define), and every gauge has its own settable red warning light. A central control box takes signals from the vehicle harness and connects the included solid-state senders, which keeps installation simple. Full details are in the sections below.
>
> *Bezel not included; it's shown for presentation. The system uses your OEM or reproduction bezel.

Every gauge has its own settable red warning light. A central control box takes signals from the car's harness and connects the included solid-state senders.

Indicators stay hidden until they light up (turn signals, high beam, check engine, cruise control, parking brake and two you define), and every gauge has its own settable red warning light. A central control box takes signals from the vehicle harness and connects the included solid-state senders, which keeps installation simple. Full details are in the sections below.

## VHX-66C-CVL: 1966-67 Chevy Chevelle/El Camino VHX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1966-67-chevy-chevelle-el-camino-vhx-instruments-p798.html` (product 798)

### Rewrite

Six analog readouts and one large LCD message center bolt into a 1966-67 Chevelle or El Camino with this VHX system. The needles light up over backlit faces, and the message center shows trip meters, a 0-60 timer and quarter-mile times.

It's a direct bolt-in replacement that uses an original or reproduction bezel, sold separately. A replacement bezel, part 610088, is listed with this kit. Faces come in black alloy, silver alloy or a carbon fiber look, in blue or red. A metric version is a special order.

Stepper motors move the needles, and the sensors are solid state. The included pushbuttons handle calibration. VHX is engineered and built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, with a limited lifetime warranty.

### Original

> The first major redesign of the new A-body platform saw many styling changes, and the instrument system was among the updates. VHX-66C-CVL is a direct bolt-in replacement using an original or reproduction bezel (available separately) that fits six analog readouts with the addition on a single, large LCD message center.
>
> VHX gives you real needles that light up, backlit faces and easy-to-read LCD message centers. Underneath are micro-controlled precision stepper motors, solid-state sensors and displays you can customize. Like every Dakota Digital system, VHX is engineered and built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, and carries a limited lifetime warranty.
>

Bright vacuum fluorescent displays show speed, rpm, oil pressure, water temp, fuel and volts in blue or teal. The message centers add an odometer, two trip meters, a 0-60 timer and quarter-mile time and trap speed.

> Series III systems use bright vacuum fluorescent displays: easy to read, accurate, and built for years of trouble-free driving.
>

## VFD3-60C-PU: 1960-63 Chevy Pickup Digital Instrument System

Page: `redesign/shop/1960-63-chevy-pickup-digital-instrument-system-p155.html` (product 155)

### Rewrite

Drop this Series III system into the OEM bezel of a 1960-63 Chevy pickup and you get six digital gauges. The vacuum fluorescent readouts glow in blue or teal and are easy to read, and the message centers add trip meters, a 0-60 timer and quarter-mile times.

It uses your OEM or reproduction bezel, which isn't included. It doesn't fit a GMC of the same years. A metric version is a special order.

Water temperature, oil pressure and speed sensors are included, each with a 10 ft harness. The included pushbuttons calibrate the speedometer and fuel gauge, set the cylinder count and scroll through the message centers.

## RTX-54C-PU-X: 1954 Chevy Pickup RTX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1954-chevy-pickup-rtx-instruments-p1231.html` (product 1231)

### Rewrite

Two round gauges with chrome-plated billet aluminum bezels give a 1954 Chevy pickup a stock look. Inside the sweep of the OEM-styled 120 mph speedometer is a bar-type tachometer, so the vintage feel stays while the system does it all. A single TFT message center brings up extra data when you want it.

The machined aluminum and chrome bezel is included. Oil pressure, water temp, fuel and volts round out the readings. A metric version is a special order.

Every RTX housing is CNC machined, and the electronics are designed and built in-house. Water temperature, oil pressure and speed sensors come with 10 ft harnesses. A two-sided rocker switch handles setup, and the LED backlighting comes on in the colors you choose.

### Original

> Stunning dual-round arrangement features chrome-plated billet aluminum bezels with a decidedly stock look! The OEM-styled 120mph speedometer is not alone; there’s bar-type tachometer nestled inside the sweep, giving you a fully functional instrument system without disturbing the vintage vibe. A single TFT message center brings up additional data for those who need just a little more!
>
> RTX keeps the stock look and adds the late-model features you want. The layout, faces and indicators follow the original design so closely that most people will take it for factory, right up until you turn the key and the LED backlighting comes on in your colors.
>
> Every RTX housing is CNC machined, and the electronics are designed and built in-house. The high-definition TFT message centers show just about any reading you want, and one central control box keeps installation and setup simple. Full details are in the sections below.
>
> *Machined aluminum and chrome bezel included.

## HDX-64P-GTO: 1964-67 Pontiac GTO/ LeMans/ Tempest HDX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1964-67-pontiac-gto-lemans-tempest-hdx-instruments-p1077.html` (product 1077)

### Rewrite

Two TFT message centers and six analog gauge sweeps fill the four openings of a 1964-67 Pontiac A-body dash, so you can view nearly any reading you want. Order black or silver alloy faces, and pick your own lighting colors.

The fully machined housing attaches directly to the stock gauge bezel of the GTO, LeMans and Tempest, including the 1963 Tempest. The bezel, trim and fascia aren't included. A metric version is a special order.

Set it up with the built-in touch buttons or the Bluetooth app for iPhone and Android. Every gauge has its own settable red warning light. A central control box takes signals from the car's harness and connects the included solid-state senders, which keeps installation simple.

### Original

> This instrument system is lookin’ fine, and installation is a snap! The fully machined instrument housing attaches directly to the stock gauge bezel found in 1964-67 Pontiac A bodies (including '63 Tempest!). Two TFT message centers and six analog gauge sweeps will the quartet of openings, allowing the driver to view nearly any parameters desired. Order with your choice of black or silver face styling.
>
> HDX comes with black or silver alloy faces, and you choose separate lighting colors for the readings, needles and message centers. Set it up with the built-in touch buttons or the Bluetooth app for iPhone and Android. Needle hubs are black nickel-plated spun aluminum to match the buttons. Indicators stay hidden until they light up (turn signals, high beam, check engine, cruise control, parking brake and two you define), and every gauge has its own settable red warning light. A central control box takes signals from the vehicle harness and connects the included solid-state senders, which keeps installation simple. Full details are in the sections below.
>
> *Bezel, trim and fascia not included; they're shown for presentation. The system uses your OEM or reproduction bezel.

## RTX-66C-NOV-X: 1966-67 Chevy Nova RTX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1966-67-chevy-nova-rtx-instruments-p1235.html` (product 1235)

### Rewrite

Showroom stock is how this 1966-67 Nova dash looks with the key off. Admirers have to look twice to spot the full-color TFT message centers and the LED bar graphs for oil pressure and volts. With it on, the driver gets clear readings for speed, rpm, water temp and fuel too.

It's a direct fit for your stock 1966-67 Nova gauge bezel, OEM or reproduction, which isn't included. Drag racers, autocross fans and street machiners all use it. A metric version is a special order.

Every RTX housing is CNC machined, and the electronics are designed and built in-house. Water temperature, oil pressure and speed sensors come with 10 ft harnesses. A two-sided rocker switch handles setup, and one central control box keeps installation simple.

### Original

> The Chevy II/Nova has developed a fierce following of drag racers, pro-touring/autocross crazies and street machiners. Whichever category you fall in to, this direct-fit instrument system will keep your interior looking showroom-stock with a myriad of sneaky, 21-Century features and upgrades. Designed to fit your stock 1966-67 Chevy Nova gauge bezel, admirers will have to look twice to see the full-color TFT message centers and LED bar graph displays for oil pressure and volts. All were designed to maintain a stock appearance with the key off, but provide incredible visibility for the driver while in use.
>
> RTX keeps the stock look and adds the late-model features you want. The layout, faces and indicators follow the original design so closely that most people will take it for factory, right up until you turn the key and the LED backlighting comes on in your colors.
>
> Every RTX housing is CNC machined, and the electronics are designed and built in-house. The high-definition TFT message centers show just about any reading you want, and one central control box keeps installation and setup simple. Full details are in the sections below.
>
> *Bezel not included; it's shown for presentation. The system uses your OEM or reproduction bezel.

## HDX-40F: 1940 Ford Car and 1940-47 Ford Truck HDX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1940-ford-car-and-1940-47-ford-truck-hdx-instruments-p1079.html` (product 1079)

### Rewrite

A chrome-plated, machined bezel comes with this HDX kit for the iconic 1940 Ford dash. A full-color TFT message center sits in the OEM-styled layout, and you pick black or silver alloy faces with your own lighting colors.

It fits the stock dash opening of 1940 Ford cars and 1940-47 Ford pickups. The matching HLC-40F clock, sold separately, has a chrome bezel made for the stock '40 glove box. It isn't a stand-alone unit and plugs into the HDX control box. A metric version is a special order.

Water temperature, oil pressure and speed sensors come with 10 ft harnesses, and the speed sensor works with Ford or GM style cables. Set it up with the touch buttons or the Bluetooth app for iPhone and Android.

### Original

> The 1940 Ford dashboard has become an icon in its own right, finding its way into many street rods over the years. This kit comes complete with a chrome plated, machined bezel and a look that can’t be beat! The full-color TFT message center finds a home in an OEM-styled system that fits the stock dash opening of 1940 cars as well as 1940-47 Ford pickups. This HLC-40F clock is the finishing touch for your HDX installation. The machined, chrome plated bezel is designed to fit a stock ’40 glove box and the face styling matches the required parent system. Not a stand-alone unit, easy plug-in connectivity to the HDX control box leaves you no reason to skimp!
>
> HDX comes with black or silver alloy faces, and you choose separate lighting colors for the readings, needles and message centers. Set it up with the built-in touch buttons or the Bluetooth app for iPhone and Android. Needle hubs are black nickel-plated spun aluminum to match the buttons. Indicators stay hidden until they light up (turn signals, high beam, check engine, cruise control, parking brake and two you define), and every gauge has its own settable red warning light. A central control box takes signals from the vehicle harness and connects the included solid-state senders, which keeps installation simple. Full details are in the sections below.

## VFD3-88C-PU-U: 1988-91 Chevy/ GMC Pickup Digital Instrument System

Page: `redesign/shop/1988-91-chevy-gmc-pickup-digital-instrument-system-p159.html` (product 159)

### Rewrite

For 1988-91 full-size Chevy and GMC pickups with factory gauges but no factory tach, this system fits right into the OEM instrument cluster. Six digital gauges read in blue or teal, and the message centers add trip meters, a 0-60 timer and quarter-mile times.

It won't fit trucks with indicator lights instead of gauges, or trucks with a factory tach. On automatic trucks it removes the gear indicator in the stock cluster. Add the GSS-3000 gear shift sensor to show the shifter position in the display. A metric version is a special order.

Water temperature, oil pressure and speed sensors come with 10 ft harnesses. The included pushbuttons handle calibration and the message centers.

### Original

> Full 6 Gauge Instrument System fits into OEM instrument cluster for 1988- 91 Chevy & GMC Fullsize Pickup.
>
> Fits directly into existing instrument cluster of 1988- 91 Fullsize trucks which have factory gauges (not indicator lights) but do NOT have a factory tachometer. *System eliminates the gear indicator found in the OEM instrument cluster of models which had an automatic transmission. To add a shifter detent readout within the VFD3 display, add GSS-3000 Gear Shift Sensor.

## HDX-2018: HDX-2018: Universal 4.4" x 11.4" Rounded Rectangle, Analog HDX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/hdx-2018-universal-4-4-x-11-4-rounded-rectangle-analog-hdx-i-p1074.html` (product 1074)

### Rewrite

For a custom interior, this universal HDX cluster fits six analog gauge readings and a large, full-color TFT message center. It measures 11.4 inches wide and 4.4 inches tall and sits just two inches deep behind the dash. Pick black or silver alloy faces and separate lighting colors for the readings, needles and message centers.

The cutout is 11 by 4 inches. The housing and built-in front bezel are billet aluminum with show chrome plating. It works with BIM-series modules, and the sending units are supplied.

Set it up with the built-in touch buttons or the Bluetooth app for Apple and Android. Indicators stay hidden until they light, and each gauge has its own settable red warning light. A central control box takes signals from the vehicle harness.

### Original

> Checking in at 11.4” wide and 4.4” tall (11" x 4" cutout), this all-in-one instrument system is a tremendous choice for your custom interior project. Total depth behind the dash is just two inches, a real feat considering the six analog gauge readings plus a large, full-color TFT message center. Integrated capacitive touch switches and supplied sending units, plus BIM-series compatibility makes this package your instrumentation solution. Housing and integrated front bezel are billet aluminum and show-chrome plated. Available with your choice of black or silver alloy gauge-face styling, each HDX system allows the user to select independent illumination colors for the gauge readings and needles, as well as the message centers. Calibration and personal settings are manipulated with the built-in, capacitive-touch buttons, or the available Bluetooth app for Apple and Android devices. Each needle hub is black nickel plated spun-aluminum to match the buttons. All HDX instrument systems feature dead-faced indicators (turn signals, high beam, check engine, cruise control, parking brake, plus two user-definable), and a red, settable warning light for each gauge. With our central control box, installation is greatly simplified with vehicle-harness sourced signals and supplied, solid state sending units connecting cleanly to their respective terminal.

Page: `redesign/shop/vhx-1013-universal-triple-round-analog-vhx-instrument-p725.html` (product 725)

### Rewrite

Three round gauges in the layout the '57 Chevy made popular give a custom dash plenty of flexibility. The large gauge measures 6.25 inches overall, and the two small ones are 3 inches. Lit needles sit over backlit faces, and LCD message centers show trip meters and a 0-60 timer.

Cutouts are 5.9 inches for the large gauge and 2.75 inches for the small ones. Chromed billet aluminum housings and bezels are part of the package. Faces come in black alloy, silver alloy or a carbon fiber look, in blue or red. A metric version is a special order.

Stepper motors drive the needles, and the included pushbuttons handle calibration. Water temperature, oil pressure and speed sensors come with 10 ft harnesses. VHX is built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, with a limited lifetime warranty.

### Original

> Made popular by the ‘57 Chevy, this contemporary triple gauge layout lends itself to an enormous amount of flexbility. With the larger instrument measuring 6.25” overall (5.9" cutout) and the two smaller gauges at 3” (2.75" cutout), this is one distinctive choice. Chromed billet aluminum housings and bezels round out the package.
>
> VHX gives you real needles that light up, backlit faces and easy-to-read LCD message centers. Underneath are micro-controlled precision stepper motors, solid-state sensors and displays you can customize. Like every Dakota Digital system, VHX is engineered and built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, and carries a limited lifetime warranty.

## VHX-63C-IMP: 1963-64 Chevy Impala VHX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1963-64-chevy-impala-vhx-instruments-p822.html` (product 822)

### Rewrite

The big instrument housing of a 1963-64 Impala holds six analog instruments and one large LCD message center that shows two readings at once. All the indicators are built into the face and stay hidden until they light.

It also fits the Biscayne and Bel Air. The bezel isn't included, so use an OEM or reproduction one. Cars with factory A/C need some changes to the ductwork on the stock gauge bezel. Faces come in black alloy, silver alloy or a carbon fiber look, in blue or red. A metric version is a special order.

Lit needles ride on stepper motors over backlit faces. The included pushbuttons handle calibration. VHX is engineered and built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, with a limited lifetime warranty.

### Original

> An innovative design solution for the large instrument housing** found in the 1963-64 Impala, VHX-63C-IMP incorporates six analog instruments with one, large LCD message center capable of displaying two readings at one time. All indicators are integrated into the instrument face and remain hidden until activated. Also fits Biscayne and Bel Air.
>
> VHX gives you real needles that light up, backlit faces and easy-to-read LCD message centers. Underneath are micro-controlled precision stepper motors, solid-state sensors and displays you can customize. Like every Dakota Digital system, VHX is engineered and built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, and carries a limited lifetime warranty.
>
> *Bezel not included, shown for visual/presentation purposes.System uses OEM or reproduction style bezel. **Cars with factory A/C will require modifications to the stock gauge bezel ductwork.

## VHX-61C-IMP: 1961-62 Chevy Impala VHX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1961-62-chevy-impala-vhx-instruments-p824.html` (product 824)

### Rewrite

No modification needed for a 1961-62 Impala: this VHX system fits right into the stock dash. The upper display holds the speedometer, voltmeter, fuel level and message centers, and the lower displays hold the tach, oil pressure and water temp.

The upper display reuses the stock trim ring. The lower ones come with new machined, chromed aluminum housings. The dash and the chrome speedometer trim and bezel aren't included. Faces come in black alloy, silver alloy or a carbon fiber look, in blue or red. A metric version is a special order.

Stepper motors drive the lit needles, and the sensors are solid state. The included pushbuttons handle calibration. VHX is built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, and carries a limited lifetime warranty.

## VFD3-92C-PU-U: 1992-94 Chevy/ GMC Pickup/ Suburban/ Tahoe/ Yukon Digital Instrument System

Page: `redesign/shop/1992-94-chevy-gmc-pickup-suburban-tahoe-yukon-digital-instru-p160.html` (product 160)

### Rewrite

The OEM instrument cluster of 1992-94 full-size Chevy and GMC pickups, Suburbans, Tahoes and Yukons takes six digital gauges with this system. They read in blue or teal, and the message centers add trip meters and a 0-60 timer.

It only fits trucks that have a factory tachometer. On automatic trucks it removes the gear indicator in the stock cluster. Add the GSS-3000 gear shift sensor to show the shifter position in the display. A metric version is a special order.

Water temperature, oil pressure and speed sensors come with 10 ft harnesses, and the speed sensor needs about 5 inches of clearance. The included pushbuttons handle calibration and the message centers.

### Original

> Full 6 Gauge Instrument System fits into OEM instrument cluster for 1992- 94 Chevy & GMC Fullsize Pickup, Suburban, Tahoe and Yukon dash.
>
> Fits directly into existing instrument cluster of trucks which HAVE a factory tachometer.
>
> *System eliminates the gear indicator found in the OEM instrument cluster of models which had an automatic transmission. To add the a shifter detent readout within the VFD3 display, add GSS-3000 Gear Shift Sensor.

## VFD3-57C: 1957 Chevy Digital Instrument System

Page: `redesign/shop/1957-chevy-digital-instrument-system-p164.html` (product 164)

### Rewrite

A 1957 Chevy dash gets six bright vacuum fluorescent gauges in its OEM bezel with this Series III system. They read in blue or teal and are easy to see. The message centers add trip meters and a 0-60 timer.

The bezel isn't included. Reproduction chrome bezels and a VFD clock kit with a new clock housing are sold separately. A metric version is a special order.

Water temperature, oil pressure and speed sensors come with 10 ft harnesses, and the speed sensor needs about 5 inches of clearance. Mount the included pushbuttons where you can reach them to calibrate the speedometer and fuel gauge.

### Rewrite

The one-year-only 1967 Camaro console gauge layout finally gets accuracy to match its looks. This RTX package installs in place of the original instruments and adds twin TFT message centers, and it even replicates the analog clock.

It uses the stock bezels and gauge housings from your '67 Camaro. The dash and console bezels aren't supplied. It's a complete package and can't be split up. A metric version is a special order.

Every RTX housing is CNC machined, and the electronics are designed and built in-house. Water temperature, oil pressure and speed sensors come with 10 ft harnesses. A two-sided rocker switch handles setup, and the LED backlighting comes on in your colors.

## HDX-69C-CAC: 1969 Camaro with Console gauges, HDX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1969-camaro-with-console-gauges-hdx-instruments-p1081.html` (product 1081)

### Rewrite

A 1969 Camaro with the center console option gets two TFT message centers and six analog readings with this HDX system. Choose black or silver faces. Billet aluminum trim rings, powdercoated black, replace the original lenses and indicator strips.

The main and satellite instruments fit the existing plastic bezels and housings. The main bezel and the console gauge bezel, trim and fascia are sold separately, and a 1969 Camaro instrument carrier is listed. The console instruments are part of the main system and can't be used alone. A metric version is a special order.

Set it up with the touch buttons or the Bluetooth app for iPhone and Android. A central control box connects the included solid-state senders.

### Original

> A highly sought-after option for a sought-after car, the center console upgrade for 1969 Camaro is an excellent candidate for HDX! Two TFT message centers compliment six analog readings, and you can choose from black or silver gauge face color. Original instrument lenses and indicator strips are eliminated with our billet aluminum, black powder coated trim rings. Main and satellite instruments fit into existing plastic bezels/housings; main bezel sold separately. Center console instruments are part of the main system and cannot be used stand-alone.
>
> HDX comes with black or silver alloy faces, and you choose separate lighting colors for the readings, needles and message centers. Set it up with the built-in touch buttons or the Bluetooth app for iPhone and Android. Needle hubs are black nickel-plated spun aluminum to match the buttons. Indicators stay hidden until they light up (turn signals, high beam, check engine, cruise control, parking brake and two you define), and every gauge has its own settable red warning light. A central control box takes signals from the vehicle harness and connects the included solid-state senders, which keeps installation simple. Full details are in the sections below.
>
> *Center Console Gauge Bezel/ Trim/ Facia not included, shown for visual/presentation purposes. Speedometer and Tachometer fit into OEM or reproduction style gauge carrier/ bezel/ trim/ facia.

Machined aluminum, powdercoated bezels come with it, and the electronics are water resistant for topless trail rides. Faces come in black alloy, silver alloy or a carbon fiber look, in blue or red. A metric version is a special order.

Water temperature, oil pressure and speed sensors come with 10 ft harnesses, and the included pushbuttons handle calibration. Stepper motors drive the needles. VHX is built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, with a limited lifetime warranty.

> The most modified vehicle in the world can also enjoy the perks of a direct-fit instrument system that boasts a classic look and modern technology. Taking the place of the original three gauges with machined aluminum and powder coated bezels, the electronics are water resistant for topless trail rides.
>
> VHX gives you real needles that light up, backlit faces and easy-to-read LCD message centers. Underneath are micro-controlled precision stepper motors, solid-state sensors and displays you can customize. Like every Dakota Digital system, VHX is engineered and built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, and carries a limited lifetime warranty.

## VHX-53C: 1953-54 Chevy Car VHX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1953-54-chevy-car-vhx-instruments-p844.html` (product 844)

### Rewrite

This all-new VHX system fits directly into a stock or reproduction gauge bezel of a 1953-54 Chevy car. Real needles light up over backlit faces, and LCD message centers add trip meters and a 0-60 timer.

Because of the unique dash in these cars, some steel in the stock dash needs trimming to make room for the larger cluster. The bezel isn't included. Faces come in black or silver alloy, and you can order blue, red or white. A metric version is a special order.

Water temperature, oil pressure and speed sensors are included, each with a 10 ft harness. Stepper motors drive the needles, and the included pushbuttons handle calibration. VHX is built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, with a limited lifetime warranty.

## VHX-67C-CAC: 1967 Camaro with Console gauges VHX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1967-camaro-with-console-gauges-vhx-instruments-p848.html` (product 848)

### Rewrite

A first-gen 1967 Camaro with console gauges gets modern technology and styling with this VHX system. Fully lit needles, backlit faces and LCD message centers cover both the dash and the console.

It attaches directly to the back of the stock instrument and center console housings, so fitment is simple. Faces come in black alloy, silver alloy or a carbon fiber look, in blue or red. A metric version is a special order.

Micro-controlled stepper motors move the needles, and the sensors are solid state. Water temperature, oil pressure and speed sensors come with 10 ft harnesses, and the included pushbuttons handle calibration. It's built in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, with a limited lifetime warranty.

### Original

> The first generation Camaro began an American legacy. The new VHX system will modernize your ride with the latest technology and styling. Attaching directly to the back side of the stock instrument and center console housings, the fitment couldn’t be simpler.
>
> Fully lit needles, backlit faces, and highly visible LCD message centers are a few of the things that you can see which set the VHX Series apart from yesterday’s traditional approach to instrumentation. A few of the features which you don’t normally see are the micro-controlled precision stepper motors, solid state sensors for unparalleled accuracy, and user-customizable display feedback, all supported by an unrivaled limited lifetime warranty which has become the standard for Dakota Digital instrumentation systems . Completely engineered and manufactured at our facility in Sioux Falls, South Dakota, the VHX Series has completely revolutionized the instrumentation industry! System fit stock or reproduction instrument and center console housings (not included).

# Batch 18

Each product: the rewrite, the original description as it was on the page, and every fact in the rewrite with where it came from. Sources: Description (the main copy), Description footnote (the * notes), Specifications / Extra Items / Pictures tabs, Options and Versions in the buy box, and the product title. ## RTX-64F-FAL-X: 1964-65 Ford Falcon, Ranchero and Mustang RTX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1964-65-ford-falcon-ranchero-and-mustang-rtx-instruments-p1272.html` (product 1272)

### Rewrite

The unique cluster in a 1964-65 Falcon or non-Pony Mustang gets a direct-fit RTX upgrade with an OEM look. A hidden LED bar-graph tach sits above the wide speedometer sweep, and twin full-color TFT message centers flank it.

It uses the stock front bezel, OEM or reproduction, which isn't included. It also fits the Ranchero. A metric version is a special order.

Every RTX housing is CNC machined, and the electronics are designed and built in-house. Water temperature, oil pressure and speed sensors come with 10 ft harnesses, and the speed sensor works with Ford or GM style cables. A two-sided rocker switch handles setup, and the LED backlighting comes on in your colors.

### Original

> The littlest sibling to the Galaxie and Fairlane, the Ford Falcon is still winning the hearts of racers and builders. The unique cluster found in the 1964-65 Falcon and non-Pony Mustang requires a unique solution, so our heroic engineering department created this RTX system as a direct-fit upgrade that makes use of the stock front bezel to add late-model features with an OEM look. A sneaky LED bar graph-type tachometer lives above the wide speedometer sweep, which is flanked by twin full-color TFT message centers.
>
> RTX keeps the stock look and adds the late-model features you want. The layout, faces and indicators follow the original design so closely that most people will take it for factory, right up until you turn the key and the LED backlighting comes on in your colors.
>
> Every RTX housing is CNC machined, and the electronics are designed and built in-house. The high-definition TFT message centers show just about any reading you want, and one central control box keeps installation and setup simple. Full details are in the sections below.
>
> *Bezel not included, shown for visual/presentation purposes only. System uses OEM or reproduction style bezel.

## HDX-71C-CAP: 1971-76 Chevy Caprice/ Impala HDX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1971-76-chevy-caprice-impala-hdx-instruments-p1084.html` (product 1084)

### Rewrite

Designed as a drop-in for the 1971-76 Chevy Impala and Caprice, this HDX system includes the sending units it needs. A large TFT message center lets you customize extra readings, and you set the lighting colors to match any paint scheme.

Choose black or silver alloy faces when you order. The bezel isn't included, so use an OEM or reproduction one. A metric version is a special order.

Calibrate it with the built-in touch buttons or the Bluetooth app for Apple and Android. The needle hubs are black nickel-plated spun aluminum, indicators stay hidden until they light, and every gauge has its own settable red warning light. A central control box takes signals from the car's harness.

### Original

> Shut down the block with this one! Designed as a drop-in replacement for 1971-76 Chevy Impala and Caprice, required sending units are included for a painless installation. When ordering, choose from black or silver gauge background; the illumination colors are user-adjustable to match any paint scheme. A large TFT message center allows full customization of auxiliary readings, keeping the driver in tune with under hood happenings. Available with your choice of black or silver alloy gauge-face styling, each HDX system allows the user to select independent illumination colors for the gauge readings and needles, as well as the message centers. Calibration and personal settings are manipulated with the built-in, capacitive-touch buttons, or the available Bluetooth app for Apple and Android devices. Each needle hub is black nickel plated spun-aluminum to match the buttons. All HDX instrument systems feature dead-faced indicators (turn signals, high beam, check engine, cruise control, parking brake, plus two user-definable), and a red, settable warning light for each gauge. With our central control box, installation is greatly simplified with vehicle-harness sourced signals and supplied, solid state sending units connecting cleanly to their respective terminal.
>
> *Bezel not included; it's shown for presentation. The system uses your OEM or reproduction bezel.

## RTX-70P-FIR-X: 1970-81 Pontiac Firebird RTX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1970-81-pontiac-firebird-rtx-instruments-p1280.html` (product 1280)

### Rewrite

The dash is part of why the second-gen Firebird has a cult following, and this direct-fit RTX package keeps its original styling. Matte black machined aluminum bezels hold full-color TFT message centers and precision stepper motor gauges, plus an analog clock.

The kit has five gauges. In a three-gauge dash, you can leave out the oil and temp gauges and still see that data in the message centers. It uses your OEM or reproduction bezel, which isn't included. A metric version is a special order.

Every RTX housing is CNC machined, and the electronics are designed and built in-house. Water temperature, oil pressure and speed sensors come with 10 ft harnesses. A two-sided rocker switch handles setup, and the LED backlighting comes on in your colors.

### Original

> Pontiac’s take on the second gen pony car has risen to cult status over the decades, and surely the dashboard has played a part in the popularity. For this direct fit package, we studied the stock instruments to capture the original styling while adding a fist full of modern features. Machined aluminum gauge bezels are given the matte black treatment before the electronics, including full-color TFT message centers and precision stepper motors, are stuffed inside. Kit includes five gauges; forgo the oil and temp gauges in your three-gauge dash and the data will still be available in the message centers!
>
> RTX keeps the stock look and adds the late-model features you want. The layout, faces and indicators follow the original design so closely that most people will take it for factory, right up until you turn the key and the LED backlighting comes on in your colors.
>
> Every RTX housing is CNC machined, and the electronics are designed and built in-house. The high-definition TFT message centers show just about any reading you want, and one central control box keeps installation and setup simple. Full details are in the sections below.
>
> *Bezel not included; it's shown for presentation. The system uses your OEM or reproduction bezel.

### Facts and sources

| Fact in the rewrite | Source | What the page says |

## RTX-49F-X: 1949-50 Ford Car RTX Instrument System

Page: `redesign/shop/1949-50-ford-car-rtx-instrument-system-p1283.html` (product 1283)

### Rewrite

It looks stock, but this direct-fit RTX system for the 1949-50 Ford car marries the original gauge design with a full-color TFT message center and LED backlighting. A spun aluminum housing holds it all together, and it talks to nearly any drivetrain.

A chrome-plated billet aluminum bezel comes with it. A matching RLC clock is sold separately. A metric version is a special order.

Every RTX housing is CNC machined, and the electronics are designed and built in-house. Water temperature, oil pressure and speed sensors come with 10 ft harnesses, and the speed sensor works with Ford or GM style cables. A two-sided rocker switch handles setup, and one central control box keeps installation simple.

### Original

> How about a direct-fit instrument upgrade that looks stock, comes with a chrome plated, billet aluminum bezel, and will talk to nearly any drivetrain combination in your 1949-50 Ford car? After studying up on the design of the stock gauge, we went to work marrying the look with a full color TFT message center, LED backlighting and spun up an aluminum housing to keep it all together. The result is decidedly stock, yet modern in all the right places.
>
> RTX keeps the stock look and adds the late-model features you want. The layout, faces and indicators follow the original design so closely that most people will take it for factory, right up until you turn the key and the LED backlighting comes on in your colors.
>
> Every RTX housing is CNC machined, and the electronics are designed and built in-house. The high-definition TFT message centers show just about any reading you want, and one central control box keeps installation and setup simple. Full details are in the sections below.

## HDX-2060: HDX-2060: Universal 6 Gauge Round, Analog HDX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/hdx-2060-universal-6-gauge-round-analog-hdx-instruments-p1088.html` (product 1088)

### Rewrite

Updating an existing panel or building one from scratch? This universal six-gauge HDX set gives you plenty of flexibility. The speedometer, tach and full-color TFT message center each drop into 3-3/8 inch holes, and the matching satellite gauges use 2-1/16 inch holes.

It needs 2 inches of depth behind the panel. Machined mounting cups hold the gauges to the panel with the supplied thumb nuts. Large gauges measure 3.55 inches overall and small ones 2.225 inches. Faces come in black or silver alloy.

Calibrate it with the built-in touch buttons or the Bluetooth app for Apple and Android. Indicators stay hidden until they light, each gauge has its own settable red warning light, and a central control box connects the supplied senders.

### Original

> HDX technology in a timeless arrangement! Whether you have an existing panel that needs to be updated, or you’re scratch building your dream, this six-gauge set offers the utmost in flexibility! The speedometer/tach and TFT message center each drop into 3 3/8” holes, while their matching satellites spec out at 2 1/16”; the HDX-2060 requires 2” of depth behind your panel. Machined mounting cups secure the gauges to your panel, attached with supplied thumb nuts. That full-color TFT message center can display as much information as you like!
>
> Available with your choice of black or silver alloy gauge-face styling, each HDX system allows the user to select independent illumination colors for the gauge readings and needles, as well as the message centers. Calibration and personal settings are manipulated with the built-in, capacitive-touch buttons, or the available Bluetooth app for Apple and Android devices. Each needle hub is black nickel plated spun-aluminum to match the buttons. All HDX instrument systems feature dead-faced indicators (turn signals, high beam, check engine, cruise control, parking brake, plus two user-definable), and a red, settable warning light for each gauge. With our central control box, installation is greatly simplified with vehicle-harness sourced signals and supplied, solid state sending units connecting cleanly to their respective terminal. Large gauge cutout size measure 3.35", and 3.55" overall. Small gauge cutout size measures 2.0625", and 2.225" overall.

The OEM bezel of a 1967 Chevy Impala or Caprice holds six bright vacuum fluorescent gauges with this Series III system. They read in blue or teal and are easy to see. The message centers add trip meters and a 0-60 timer.

## RTX-84B-REG-X: 1984-87 Buick Regal and Grand National RTX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1984-87-buick-regal-and-grand-national-rtx-instruments-p1294.html` (product 1294)

### Rewrite

A large, sweeping horizontal speedometer recalls the Buick design in this stock-looking cluster for the Regal, T-Type and Grand National. A bar-graph tach sits above it, hidden analog oil pressure and water temp gauges sit at the edges, and two programmable TFT message centers sit in between. A digital voltmeter makes six gauges.

The bezel isn't included. Adding the SEN-09-7 puts boost in the message center, and a Grand National version with it is listed. Grand National ignitions may need the SGI-17 for the tach. Metric versions are a special order.

Every RTX housing is CNC machined, and the electronics are designed and built in-house. Water temperature, oil pressure and speed sensors come with 10 ft harnesses, and one central control box keeps installation simple.

### Original

> A fully-functional, stock looking gauge cluster for your Regal, T-Type or Grand National was the goal of the Retrotech Series. A large sweeping horizontal speedometer is reminiscent of the Buick design, with the bar-graph tachometer perfectly matching what you’d expect to see. Hidden analog Oil Pressure and Water Temperature gauges reside on the perimeter, while a pair of programmable TFT Digital Message Centers sit between them and the familiar speedometer. A digital voltmeter rounds out this 6-gauge package, offering you the features and technology of today.
>
> RTX keeps the stock look and adds the late-model features you want. The layout, faces and indicators follow the original design so closely that most people will take it for factory, right up until you turn the key and the LED backlighting comes on in your colors.
>
> Every RTX housing is CNC machined, and the electronics are designed and built in-house. The high-definition TFT message centers show just about any reading you want, and one central control box keeps installation and setup simple. Full details are in the sections below.
>
> *Bezel not included, shown for visual/presentation purposes. **SEN-09-7 adds Boost in to the message center. ***Grand National ignition systems may require the SGI-17 to support the tachometer connection.

## RTX-77C-CAP-X: 1977-90 Chevy Caprice/ Impala & 1984-86 Pontiac Parisienne RTX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1977-90-chevy-caprice-impala-and-1984-86-pontiac-parisienne--p1298.html` (product 1298)

### Rewrite

The Box Chevy suits an understated stock look, and this RTX system keeps it. It's a direct bolt-in with color-changing backlighting, full-color digital screens and mobile-app compatibility.

It fits 1977-90 Chevy B-bodies and the 1984-86 Pontiac Parisienne, in the stock dash opening. The front dash bezel isn't included. Some wiring is needed to finish the conversion, and setup is easy with a stock drivetrain or an LS swap. A metric version is a special order.

Every RTX housing is CNC machined, and the electronics are designed and built in-house. Water temperature, oil pressure and speed sensors come with 10 ft harnesses. A two-sided rocker switch handles setup, and one central control box keeps installation simple.

### Original

> The Box Chevy is a great candidate for an understated stock theme! The RTX series is the latest from Dakota Digital, focused on retaining that stock look without sacrificing the late-model features you love. Every effort has been put forth to incorporate OEM design elements regarding the layout, face styling and indicators. A casual glance may dismiss these as original equipment, but once you hit the switch and the LED backlighting comes on in your favorite color scheme, all doubts will be removed.
>
> Every RTX housing is CNC machined, and the electronics are designed and built in-house. The high-definition TFT message centers show just about any reading you want, and one central control box keeps installation and setup simple. Full details are in the sections below.

## HDX-54C-PU: 1954 Chevy Pickup HDX Instruments

Page: `redesign/shop/1954-chevy-pickup-hdx-instruments-p1092.html` (product 1092)

### Rewrite

Skip adapter rings and "good enough." Custom-machined billet aluminum bezels fit the stock dash of 1954 and 1955 First Series Chevy pickups, with six analog instruments and twin TFT message centers. Choose black or silver alloy faces.

Mounting hardware is supplied. It doesn't fit GMC. A metric version is a special order.

Set it up with the built-in touch buttons or the Bluetooth app for iPhone and Android, and pick your lighting colors. Every gauge has its own settable red warning light. Water temperature, oil pressure and speed sensors come with 10 ft harnesses, and a central control box keeps the wiring simple.

### Original

> Instead of adapter rings and a “good enough” finished product, why not go for a tailor-made instrumentation upgrade? Custom machined billet aluminum bezels fit the stock dash in 1954 and 1955 First Series Chevy pickups (sorry, not GMC) and offer six analog instruments with twin TFT message centers. Supplied mounting hardware makes this a slam-dunk in your choice of black or silver face styling.
>
> HDX comes with black or silver alloy faces, and you choose separate lighting colors for the readings, needles and message centers. Set it up with the built-in touch buttons or the Bluetooth app for iPhone and Android. Needle hubs are black nickel-plated spun aluminum to match the buttons. Indicators stay hidden until they light up (turn signals, high beam, check engine, cruise control, parking brake and two you define), and every gauge has its own settable red warning light. A central control box takes signals from the vehicle harness and connects the included solid-state senders, which keeps installation simple. Full details are in the sections below.
